# 《说春秋》之一《齐楚崛起》第21章到第30章中英对照

说明：
- 本文件按源文本从 `第63节` 起整理，计划对应第二十一章到第三十章。
- 当前已完成第二十一章到第三十章，源 txt 已全部完成。
- 排版规则为“一句中文，一句英文”；中文行末保留两个空格，便于 Markdown 预览换行。
- 英文以自然流畅为主，较难词汇在首次出现处标注中文义。
- 遇到值得讲解的长句，会补充固定样式语法说明。
- 保留原书真实章号，本文件按源文本顺序继续整理。

## 第二十一章 兄弟靠得住？
## Chapter 21: Can Brothers Be Trusted?

按照《周礼》。  
Under the *Rites of Zhou*,  
诸侯继承人本该是嫡长子。  
a feudal lord's heir was supposed to be his eldest legitimate（嫡出的） son.  
也就是正妻所生的长子。  
In other words, the firstborn son of the principal（正妻的） wife.  
整个西周时期。  
Throughout the Western Zhou,  
大家大体都守这规矩。  
people more or less kept to that rule.  
另有一条规矩。  
There was another rule as well.  
新君即位后。  
When a new ruler took the throne,  
要先上报王室。  
he had to report it to the royal house,  
再由王室派人。  
which would then send men  
正式任命。  
to confirm（正式确认） the appointment.  
到了春秋。  
By the Spring and Autumn period,  
嫡长子继承制开始松动。  
the system of succession（继承） by the eldest legitimate son had begun to break down,  
王室任命也没人当回事。  
and royal confirmation no longer meant much.  
从前还得等中央委任。  
In earlier days, rulers still waited for approval from the center.  
后来不用了。  
Later, they did not bother.  
自己刻块章。  
They could carve a seal for themselves  
就算登基。  
and call themselves enthroned.  
可麻烦也来了。  
But trouble came with that.  
你杀别人容易。  
It was easy to kill someone else,  
别人杀你也一样容易。  
and just as easy for someone else to kill you.  
你会刻章。  
If you could carve a seal,  
人家也会刻。  
so could everyone else.  
你刻个侯爵。  
You might carve yourself a marquis（侯爵）,  
人家还能刻个公爵。  
and someone else could just as easily carve himself a duke（公爵）.  
于是。  
So  
各国的继承人大战。  
succession wars  
一场接一场。  
broke out one after another.  
兄弟相残。  
Brothers turned on brothers,  
早就不新鲜了。  
and that was no longer rare.  
说实话。  
To be honest,  
当个诸侯并不轻松。  
being a feudal lord was no easy business.  
掉脑袋这件事。  
Losing one's head  
有时比平民还容易。  
could come even easier than it did for commoners.  
这一章里。  
In this chapter,  
就看看郑国四兄弟的命运。  
let us look at the fates of Zheng's four brothers.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`倒装`、`固定句型 so + 助动词 + 主语`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If you could carve a seal, so could everyone else.`。<br>
难点拆解：`If you could carve a seal` 是条件状语从句；后半句不是正常语序的 `everyone else could so`，而是用了倒装结构 `so could everyone else`，表示“别人也一样能”。<br>
常用短语：
- `take the throne`：即位
- `carve a seal`：刻印章
- `break down`：瓦解，失效
- `turn on`：反过来攻击

### 老大之死
### The Death of the Eldest Brother

郑厉公逃走后。  
After Duke Li of Zheng fled,  
祭足决定。  
Jizu decided  
把郑昭公请回来。  
to bring Duke Zhao of Zheng back.  
昭公在哪儿？  
Where was Duke Zhao?  
在卫国。  
In Wei,  
一待就是四年。  
where he had already spent four years.  
卫惠公很高兴。  
Duke Hui of Wei was delighted  
把昭公送回郑国。  
to send Duke Zhao back to Zheng.  
他为什么高兴？  
Why was he so happy?  
因为他以为。  
Because he thought  
自己能拿到不少酬谢。  
he would receive a handsome reward.  
可他想错了。  
But he was wrong.  
有了宋国那次教训。  
Having learned from what happened with Song,  
祭足很明白。  
Jizu understood very well  
酬谢给得再多。  
that no matter how much reward was given,  
卫国也不会满足。  
Wei would never be satisfied,  
最后还是会翻脸。  
and in the end it would still turn hostile.  
既然早晚都要翻脸。  
If things were bound to end in hostility anyway,  
那还不如。  
it was better  
一开始就别给。  
to give Wei nothing from the start.  
于是。  
So  
郑国什么也没给。  
Zheng gave nothing at all.  
中间那些客套。  
It even skipped  
也全省了。  
the polite formalities in between.  
等于从一开始。  
It was as if  
就把卫国当敌人了。  
Wei had been treated as an enemy from day one.  
卫惠公满怀希望。  
Duke Hui of Wei had been full of hope,  
结果一下子。  
only to be plunged  
跌到彻底失望。  
into utter disappointment.  
他气得要命。  
He was furious  
逢人就骂昭公。  
and bad-mouthed Duke Zhao to whoever would listen.  
其实昭公也没办法。  
In truth, Duke Zhao had no choice.  
他也不敢惹祭足。  
He did not dare offend Jizu either.  

当初是祭足把昭公赶走。  
It had been Jizu who drove Duke Zhao out in the first place.  
如今又是祭足把他请回。  
Now it was Jizu bringing him back.  
祭足自己也有点尴尬。  
Even Jizu felt embarrassed.  
好在昭公是厚道人。  
Fortunately, Duke Zhao was a generous man.  
他知道祭足当年。  
He knew that back then  
也是被形势逼的。  
Jizu had been forced by circumstances.  
所以他没抱怨。  
So he made no complaint.  
还是像从前那样。  
He still treated Jizu as before,  
尊敬他。  
respected him,  
也听他的话。  
and followed his advice.  
祭足本就心里有愧。  
Jizu already felt guilty.  
见昭公一点不记恨。  
Seeing that Duke Zhao bore no grudge at all,  
他反而更过意不去。  
he felt even more ashamed.  
他想来想去。  
After thinking it over,  
索性找个人。  
he decided to let Duke Zhao  
给昭公出出气。  
vent his anger on someone.  
于是祭足去见昭公。  
So Jizu went to see Duke Zhao.  
说着说着。  
After a little talk,  
就绕到了正题。  
he turned to the real subject.  
他把高渠弥当年。  
He recounted how Gao Qumi, back in those days,  
怎样第一个跳出来。  
had been the first to leap out  
支持废掉昭公。  
and support Zhao's overthrow,  
又怎样要亲手。  
and how he had even wanted  
去杀昭公。  
to kill Zhao himself.  
他还故意。  
Jizu even  
添油加醋。  
laid it on a little thick.  
最后压低声音说。  
At last he lowered his voice and said,  
“主公。  
"My lord,  
高渠弥不是好人。  
Gao Qumi is no good man.  
找机会杀了他吧。”  
Find a chance and kill him."  
昭公叹了口气。  
Duke Zhao sighed.  
“算了。  
"Let it go.  
过去的事。  
What is past  
就让它过去吧。  
should stay past.  
我也不喜欢他。  
I do not like him either,  
可那时他。  
but at that time  
也有他的难处。”  
he had his own difficulties."  
昭公确实宽厚。  
Duke Zhao truly was generous.  
他根本没想。  
He had no real thought  
要杀高渠弥。  
of killing Gao Qumi.  
祭足只好退一步。  
Jizu had to step back.  
“那就撤他的职。”  
"Then remove him from office."  
昭公还是不肯。  
Duke Zhao still would not agree.  
“我刚复位。  
"I have only just returned.  
一上来就撤他。  
If I strip him of office at once,  
别人会怎么想？  
what will others think?  
以后再说吧。”  
Let us speak of it later."  
祭足没法再劝。  
Jizu could say no more.  
不过他也放心了。  
But he also felt relieved.  
连高渠弥。  
If Duke Zhao held no grudge  
昭公都能不记仇。  
even against Gao Qumi,  
那对自己。  
then toward Jizu himself  
就更不会怨了。  
he would be even less resentful.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`结果状语`、`固定句型 be bound to`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If things were bound to end in hostility anyway, it was better to give Wei nothing from the start.`。<br>
难点拆解：`If things were bound to ...` 是条件状语从句；`be bound to` 表示“注定会”；后面的 `it was better to ...` 是很自然的判断句，表示“既然终究如此，不如一开始就这样做”。<br>
常用短语：
- `be bound to`：注定会
- `from the start`：从一开始
- `turn hostile`：翻脸，转为敌对
- `bad-mouth`：说坏话

昭公这人。  
Duke Zhao  
真是厚道。  
was that kind of man.  
他从卫国回来时。  
When he came back from Wei,  
还带回一个人。  
he brought someone with him.  
谁？  
Who?  
公父定叔。  
Gongfu Dingshu.  
他是谁？  
And who was he?  
他是公孙滑的儿子。  
He was the son of Gongsun Hua,  
叔段的孙子。  
and the grandson of Shuduan.  
算起来。  
By relation,  
他是昭公的侄子。  
he was Duke Zhao's nephew.  
公孙滑在卫国避难。  
Gongsun Hua had lived in exile in Wei,  
日子过得很苦。  
and his life there had been hard.  
没活多久就死了。  
He did not live long.  
只剩这个儿子。  
He left behind only this son.  
定叔那时还小。  
Dingshu was still a child then.  
东家吃一顿。  
He would get one meal at one house,  
西家挨一顿。  
and another at the next,  
日子熬得很难。  
scraping by with difficulty.  
昭公到卫国避难后。  
After Duke Zhao went to Wei in exile,  
听说他过得可怜。  
he heard how poor the boy's life was.  
就把他接来同住。  
So he took him in.  
这次回郑国。  
When he returned to Zheng,  
索性把他也带回。  
he simply brought him back as well  
还让他做了大夫。  
and made him a senior officer.  

再说郑厉公。  
Now as for Duke Li,  
他跑到蔡国以后。  
after fleeing to Cai,  
和蔡侯混得不错。  
he got along well with the ruler there.  
又借到一些兵。  
He even borrowed some troops  
竟一口气。  
and in one rush  
拿下了郑国大城栎城。  
seized the major Zheng city of Licheng.  
有了根据地。  
Once he had a base,  
厉公又动起心思。  
Duke Li began plotting again.  
他和鲁桓公。  
He had always been on good terms  
一向关系不错。  
with Duke Huan of Lu.  
于是先找鲁侯。  
So he first approached the lord of Lu,  
再通过鲁侯。  
and through him  
去找宋庄公。  
reached Duke Zhuang of Song.  
他认错。  
He admitted his fault  
又保证以后。  
and promised that from now on  
欠宋国的东西。  
whatever Zheng owed Song  
都会补上。  
would be paid back in full.  
他只求一件事。  
He asked only one thing:  
请宋国帮他。  
that Song help him  
夺回郑国。  
win Zheng back.  
宋庄公是什么人？  
What sort of man was Duke Zhuang of Song?  
认钱不认人。  
He cared for money, not for people.  
一听说欠账能补。  
The moment he heard the debts would be repaid,  
他就高兴了。  
he was delighted.  
于是敌人又成朋友。  
And so enemies became friends again.  
厉公和宋庄公。  
Duke Li and Duke Zhuang  
又成了哥们。  
were brothers once more.  
再加上鲁国。  
Add Lu to that,  
又拉上卫国。  
and then Wei as well,  
就成了四国联军。  
and a four-state coalition（联军） took shape.  
他们一起进攻郑国。  
Together they attacked Zheng.  
可祭足守得很稳。  
But Jizu's defense held firm.  
四国联军打不下来。  
The coalition could not take the state  
最后各自回国。  
and eventually each returned home.  
祭足随后派甫瑕。  
Jizu then stationed（驻守） Fu Xia  
驻守大陵。  
at Daling  
专门防着厉公。  
specifically to watch Duke Li.  
厉公也只好。  
Duke Li could only  
缩在栎城里。  
stay holed up in Licheng  
盼着祭足早点死。  
and hope Jizu would die soon.  

转眼过了三年。  
In a blink, three years passed.  
昭公回郑的第三年。  
In Duke Zhao's third year back in Zheng,  
齐襄公要娶周王之女。  
Duke Xiang of Qi was going to marry a Zhou princess.  
他请鲁桓公。  
He asked Duke Huan of Lu  
做订婚主持人。  
to act as master of the betrothal.  
自从气死老丈人后。  
After having infuriated his father-in-law to death,  
鲁桓公和大舅子。  
Duke Huan and his brother-in-law  
齐襄公很快和好了。  
Duke Xiang quickly made peace.  
说不定齐襄公心里。  
For all anyone knew,  
还挺感激鲁桓公。  
Duke Xiang was even grateful to him.  

祭足一听这消息。  
As soon as Jizu heard the news,  
就知道机会来了。  
he knew an opportunity had come.  
齐国和厉公有仇。  
Qi had bad blood with Duke Li,  
而且齐僖公当年。  
and Duke Xi of Qi had once  
很欣赏昭公。  
admired Duke Zhao greatly.  
如今的齐襄公。  
The current Duke Xiang  
多半愿意。  
would probably be willing  
和郑国修好。  
to mend ties with Zheng.  
借着齐襄公。  
And through Duke Xiang,  
还能再去。  
they might also  
和鲁桓公讲和。  
make peace with Duke Huan of Lu.  
这一趟若成。  
If this trip succeeded,  
等于一下子。  
Zheng would in one stroke  
拉拢了两个大国。  
win over two great states.  
对郑国来说。  
For Zheng,  
这当然是大外交胜利。  
that would be a major diplomatic（外交的） victory.  
祭足把想法。  
Jizu laid out the idea  
对昭公一说。  
to Duke Zhao.  
昭公大喜。  
Duke Zhao was delighted  
当即派他前往。  
and sent him off at once.  

祭足带上郑国特产。  
Jizu took Zheng specialties with him  
急匆匆赶到临淄。  
and hurried to Linzi.  
齐襄公对他。  
Duke Xiang treated him  
果然非常客气。  
with great courtesy.  
一来。  
For one thing,  
他仰慕祭足名声。  
he admired Jizu's reputation.  
二来。  
For another,  
他对昭公印象很好。  
he had a fine impression of Duke Zhao.  
他爹活着时。  
While his father was alive,  
常夸“公子忽”。  
he often praised "Prince Hu."  
两人一见面。  
Once the two met,  
齐襄公对祭足的请求。  
Duke Xiang agreed to Jizu's requests  
几乎一口全答应。  
almost one after another.  
他还遗憾。  
He even regretted  
昭公没当成自己妹夫。  
that Duke Zhao had not become his brother-in-law.  
用今天的话说。  
In modern terms,  
他简直是在说。  
he was practically saying,  
“忽哥就是我偶像。”  
"Brother Hu is my idol."  

“我们一直坚持。  
"We have always upheld  
一个郑国原则。  
the one-Zheng principle.  
也坚持郑昭公。  
And we insist that Duke Zhao  
才是郑国合法君主。  
is Zheng's lawful ruler.  
栎城也是郑国。  
Licheng, too,  
不可分割的一部分。”  
is an inseparable（不可分割的） part of Zheng."  
齐襄公正说得起劲。  
Duke Xiang was speaking with great enthusiasm  
忽然有人来报。  
when someone suddenly came to report,  
“主公。  
"My lord,  
郑国政变。  
there has been a coup（政变） in Zheng.  
郑侯被杀了。”  
The lord of Zheng has been killed."  
这一来。  
With that,  
前面那些话。  
everything they had just discussed  
全白谈了。  
came to nothing.  

怎么回事？  
What had happened?  
原来祭足前脚刚走。  
As soon as Jizu left,  
高渠弥后脚就动手。  
Gao Qumi made his move.  
他和昭公。  
He and Duke Zhao  
一直就不对路。  
had never gotten along.  
而且他始终担心。  
And he had always feared  
昭公会找机会。  
that Duke Zhao would someday  
收拾自己。  
settle accounts with him.  
如今祭足出使齐国。  
Now Jizu had gone to Qi on a diplomatic mission.  
若是谈得很顺。  
If the mission went well,  
昭公的靠山。  
Duke Zhao's backing  
就会更硬。  
would grow stronger,  
自己的日子。  
while Gao Qumi's own days  
就会更难过。  
would become harder still.  
想到这里。  
Thinking this over,  
他先下手为强。  
he struck first.  
他趁昭公。  
He took advantage of a hunting trip  
去城郊打猎。  
Duke Zhao made outside the city,  
派人假扮强盗。  
sent men disguised as bandits,  
把昭公杀了。  
and had him killed.  
随后又立刻。  
After that, he immediately  
从蔡国接回。  
brought back from Cai  
郑庄公的三儿子。  
the third son of Duke Zhuang of Zheng,  
公子危。  
Prince Wei,  
让他即位。  
and put him on the throne.  

你不杀人。  
If you do not kill others,  
人就会杀你。  
they will kill you.  
从这个角度看。  
From that angle,  
郑庄公当年的看法。  
Duke Zhuang's old view  
还真没错。  
was not wrong.  
历史反复证明。  
History has proved again and again  
心慈手软的人。  
that people who are soft-hearted  
做不成政治家。  
do not become politicians.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`并列句`、`比较级`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If the mission went well, Duke Zhao's backing would grow stronger, while Gao Qumi's own days would become harder still.`。<br>
难点拆解：`If the mission went well` 是条件状语从句；主句里用 `while` 把两边的结果并列出来，一边更强，一边更难；`harder still` 是比较级加强说法。<br>
常用短语：
- `get along`：相处得好
- `mend ties`：修复关系
- `come to nothing`：落空，白谈
- `settle accounts with`：找……算账

齐襄公听到消息。  
When Duke Xiang heard the news,  
一巴掌拍在桌上。  
he slapped the table  
当场发火。  
and flew into a rage.  
“谁杀忽哥。  
"Whoever killed Brother Hu,  
我就杀谁。”  
I will kill him."  
他当即就想。  
He wanted at once  
起兵伐郑。  
to march on Zheng.  
旁边大臣连忙提醒。  
But an official beside him quickly reminded him,  
“主公快成婚了。  
"My lord, your wedding is near.  
还是等等吧。”  
Better wait."  
齐襄公想想。  
Duke Xiang thought it over  
也只好作罢。  
and had to let it go for the moment.  
齐襄公能等。  
Duke Xiang could afford to wait,  
祭足可等不起。  
but Jizu could not.  
他家里一大家子。  
He had a whole family back home,  
谁知道安不安全。  
and who knew whether they were safe?  
于是他赶紧告辞。  
So he hurriedly took his leave  
急急回国。  
and rushed back to Zheng.  

祭足一辈子。  
Jizu had spent a lifetime  
都在算计别人。  
calculating against others.  
可他终究。  
Yet in the end  
还是难免被人算计。  
he still could not escape being outplayed himself.  
那些爱动心眼的人。  
All the schemers around him  
都不是他的对手。  
were no match for him.  
偏偏最后。  
Yet he was brought down  
他却栽在。  
by two men  
两个几乎没什么心眼的人手里。  
who hardly schemed at all.  
一个是宋庄公。  
One was Duke Zhuang of Song.  
一个就是高渠弥。  
The other was Gao Qumi.  
这两个二百五。  
Those two fools  
都让他吃了大亏。  
each caught him badly off guard.  
所以说。  
So it is true  
聪明人。  
that clever men

### 老三之死
### The Death of the Third Brother

情况并没有。  
Things were not  
像祭足想得那么糟。

他的家人都安全。

someone even sent them

grain.

no one would lightly move against Jizu.  
一来他实力太强。  
First, his power was immense.

Second, his diplomatic skill was formidable（强大的）.  
就连厉公那样的人。  
Even a man like Duke Li,  
想对付祭足。  
when he tried to deal with Jizu,  
最后也把自己。  
ended up  
弄得被赶出郑国。  
driving himself out of Zheng.  
所以不论是高渠弥。  
So whether it was Gao Qumi  
还是公子危。  
or Prince Wei,  
都没想过先动祭足。  
neither thought of striking Jizu first.  
他们想的是。  
What they wanted  
把他拉过来。

So as soon as Prince Wei took the throne,  
就先给祭足家里。  
grain was sent  
送去粮食。  
to Jizu's household.  
祭足回国后。  
After Jizu returned,  
高渠弥又亲自来请。  
Gao Qumi personally came to invite him  
去见公子危。  
to meet Prince Wei.  
公子危态度很低。

一会儿问寒问暖。  
He asked after his health,  
一会儿请教政事。  
then sought advice on affairs of state.  
最后还答应。  
At last he promised  
原来的待遇不变。  
that Jizu's terms would remain unchanged  
总理还是他做。  
and that he would stay prime minister.  
到了这一步。  
At that point,  
祭足也没别的路。  
Jizu had no other road left.  
只好认了。  
He had to accept it.  
昭公已经死了。  
Duke Zhao was dead.  
死人不能复生。  
The dead do not return.  
公子危再怎么说。  
Whatever else might be said,  
总比厉公回来强些。  
Prince Wei was still better than Duke Li's return.

句子主干：主句核心是 `So whether it was Gao Qumi or Prince Wei, neither thought of striking Jizu first.`。<br>
难点拆解：`whether it was ... or ...` 表示“无论是……还是……”；前面的 `So` 承接上文原因；后面的 `neither ...` 点出两边结论一致，都不想先动祭足。<br>
常用短语：
- `no matter who`：不管谁
- `move against`：对……下手
- `win over`：拉拢
- `ask after`：问候近况

公子危在那年冬天。

总之他一定记得。”  
in any case, he surely remembers."  
公子危却不以为然。

“那都是年轻时。  
"That was when we were young.  
大家闲得没事。

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`倒装`、`并列句`、`not only ...` 结构。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `Not only did Duke Li leave Jizu's family untouched, he even took special care of Jizu's descendants.`。<br>
难点拆解：`Not only` 放在句首时，后面常用部分倒装，所以是 `did Duke Li leave`；后半句 `he even took ...` 正常语序，和前半句并列，语气上更加强了一层。<br>
常用短语：
- `wipe out`：灭掉，清除
- `leave ... untouched`：不碰，不动
- `take special care of`：特别照顾
- `serve one's own master`：各为其主

杀了祭足旧党的。  
and killed one of Jizu's old allies,  
公子阏。  
Prince Yan.  
杀完公子阏后。  
After killing Prince Yan,  
他又派人去卫国。  
he sent men to Wei  
把公父定叔找回来。  
to bring Gongfu Dingshu back.  
他说。  
He said,  
“不能让共叔。  
"We cannot let Gongshu  
在郑国绝后。”  
leave no descendants in Zheng."  
随后又安排。  
He then arranged  
定叔在十月回国。  
for Dingshu to return in the tenth month,  
还说那是吉月。  
saying it was an auspicious（吉利的） month.  
到了十月。  
When the tenth month came,  
定叔回到郑国。  
Dingshu returned to Zheng.  
厉公办了场。  
Duke Li staged  
很盛大的欢迎仪式。  
a grand welcome ceremony  
当场任命他。  
and on the spot appointed him  
做上大夫。  
a high minister.  
全国人看了。  
People across the state saw it  
都夸厉公。  
and praised Duke Li.  
“我们的国君真大度。  
"Our ruler is truly magnanimous.  
讲亲情。  
He values family ties.  
真是个好人。”  
What a good man he is."  
厉公听着只是笑。  
Duke Li merely smiled when he heard this.

当然不是。

亲弟弟他都杀。

一个堂弟？  
a cousin?

厉公显然。  
Duke Li clearly

## 第二十二章 周公主义
## Chapter 22: The Doctrine of the Duke of Zhou

周朝刚建立时。  
When the Zhou dynasty was first founded,  
武王分封诸侯。  
King Wu did not enfeoff（分封） the lords at random.  
每一步。  
Every move  
都显然想得很清楚。  
was clearly well considered.  
他的分封办法。  
His method of enfeoffment  
像老鹰捉小鸡。  
was a little like the old game of eagle and chicks.  
最危险的地方。  
The most dangerous places  
交给最强的人。  
went to the strongest men.  
最安稳的地方。  
The safest and most comfortable lands  
留给最弱的人。  
were left to the weakest.  
乍一看。  
At first glance,  
这似乎不公平。  
that may seem unfair.  
可从长远看。  
But in the long run,  
它反而很公平。  
it was remarkably fair.  
道理很简单。  
The logic was simple.  
风险越大。  
The greater the risk,  
机会也越大。  
the greater the opportunity.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`主语从句`、`转折并列句`、`比较级结构`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `What seemed unfair at first was, in the long run, remarkably fair.`。<br>
难点拆解：`What seemed unfair at first` 是主语从句，整体作主语；后面的 `was ... fair` 是主句；`in the long run` 插在中间补充时间范围；最后的 `The greater ..., the greater ...` 是很常见的比较级对应结构。<br>
常用短语：
- `at first glance`：乍一看
- `in the long run`：从长远看
- `the greater ..., the greater ...`：越……越……
- `well considered`：考虑周全的

按当时天下形势。  
Given the shape of the world at the time,  
陕西是周朝根基。  
Shaanxi was the Zhou heartland,  
而西戎还不成气候。  
while the western tribes were not yet much of a threat.  
所以周朝都城。  
So the Zhou capital  
没有迁去中原。  
was not moved into the Central Plain,  
而是留在西部。  
but remained in the west.  
南边那些蛮子国。  
As for the southern states,  
都窝在深山老林。  
they were still buried in remote mountains and forests  
一时不用多管。  
and did not yet require much attention.  
真正的大敌。  
The real enemies  
主要在两边。  
lay in two directions.  
一在东边。  
One was in the east.  
东夷力量很强。

而且忠于商朝。  
and still loyal to Shang.  
那时周的势力。  
At that time, Zhou power  
根本还到不了东海。

二在北边。

北狄来去如风。  
The northern Di moved like the wind  
破坏力很强。  
and were highly destructive（破坏性强的）.  

基于这种局面。

武王做了这样的安排。

姜太公封在齐。

在今天山东北部。

专门对付东夷。

在今天山东南部。

周朝东面的疆界。

### 孔圣人心中的圣人

### 周朝元勋

### 平定叛乱
### Putting Down the Rebellion

灭商第二年。  
In the second year after Shang fell,  
武王得了重病。  
King Wu became gravely ill.  
那时天下。  
At that moment,  
离真正安定。  
the realm was still far  
还差得很远。  
from truly being settled.  
大家都慌了。

这时周公。

写下祷词。

向祖先祈求。  
to beg the ancestors

藏进祖庙金滕匮。  
inside the metal-bound casket in the ancestral temple

绝不能泄露。

可不久之后。

武王还是去世了。

从科学角度说。

祈祷当然没用。

那是个敬鬼神的时代。  
it was an age that revered spirits and gods.  
向鬼神发这种誓。

是极严肃的事。

儿子姬诵继位。

stayed on.  
他不只做总理。  
He did not remain only as prime minister.  
他还做了摄政。  
He also became regent（摄政者）.  
也就是说。  
That is to say,  
他代替成王。  
he sat in the king's place  
管理天下。  
and governed the realm on King Cheng's behalf.  

周公一摄政。  
The moment he became regent,  
不满的人就多了。  
many people grew dissatisfied.  
有人嫉妒。  
Some were jealous.  
有人不服。  
Some refused to accept it.  
甚至直到今天。  
Even today,  
还有一些人。  
there are people  
硬说周公想篡位。  
who insist the Duke of Zhou wanted to usurp the throne.  
甚至还断言。  
Some even declare  
武王是他害死的。  
that he killed King Wu himself.  
对此作者。  
On this point, the author  
骂得很不客气。  
is not polite at all.  
他说这些人。  
He says such people  
骗稿费骗职称。  
chase fees and titles  
也该留点良心。  
with very little conscience.  

最眼红的人。  
The most jealous of all  
是老三管叔鲜。  
was the third brother, Guan Shu Xian.  
他大概觉得。  
He probably felt  
兄终弟及。  
that if one brother died,  
也该轮到自己。  
the next brother in line should be him,  
轮不到老四。  
not the fourth.  
可他没想过。  
But he never asked himself  
自己和周公。  
how large the gap  
能力差了多少。  
in ability between him and the Duke of Zhou really was.  
不管怎么说。  
In any case,  
他煽动了弟弟蔡叔度。  
he incited his younger brother Cai Shu Du  
准备造反。  
to rebel.  
更蠢的是。  
Even more foolishly,  
他还和纣王之子。  
he joined hands with  
武公禄父联手。  
Wu Geng Lufu, the son of King Zhou of Shang.  
这一联手。  
By doing that,  
等于直接承认。  
he all but declared himself  
自己就是叛军。  
a rebel.  

正式起兵前。  
Before open revolt began,  
管叔先打舆论战。  
Guan Shu first launched a battle of rumor.  
他在民间到处放话。  
He spread word everywhere  
说周公想篡位。  
that the Duke of Zhou meant to seize the throne.  
谣言传多了。  
Once a rumor is repeated enough times,  
连公卿大夫。  
even the nobles and ministers  
都开始起疑。  
begin to waver.  
可周公站得太高。  
But the Duke of Zhou stood at a height  
不是管叔这些人。  
that Guan Shu and his circle  
能看得明白的。  
could hardly imagine.  
对付这些小角色。  
Against such lesser figures,  
周公用了三招。  
the Duke of Zhou used three moves.  

第一招。  
The first move  
是牢牢抓住军队。  
was to keep a tight grip on the army  
时刻备战。  
and remain ready at all times.  
这方面他有经验。  
In this he had experience.  
伐纣时。  
He had fought in the conquest of Shang  
他就上过战场。  
and knew war firsthand.  
文武两方面。  
In both civil and military matters,  
他都很强。  
he was formidable.  
而且周朝主力。  
And the main Zhou forces  
本来就在他手里。  
were already under his hand.  

第二招。  
The second move  
是稳住三巨头。  
was to keep the three giants united.  
流言刚起来时。  
As soon as the rumors spread,  
周公就请来。  
the Duke of Zhou invited  
姜太公和召公。  
Jiang Taigong and Duke Shao  
坐下来深谈。  
to sit down for a frank talk.  
他们三人。  
Those three  
就是当时周朝。  
were the true power trio  
最核心的人物。  
of the Zhou court.  
关于召公。  
As for Duke Shao,  
史料说得不算细。  
the sources are not entirely detailed.  
他可能是周公弟弟。  
He may have been the Duke of Zhou's younger brother,  
也可能是堂兄弟。  
or perhaps a cousin,  
甚至是堂叔。  
or even an uncle by clan.  
但有一点确定。  
But one thing is certain:  
他是王族。  
he belonged to the royal house  
而且和成王关系很近。  
and stood very close to King Cheng.  
作者这里。  
Here the author  
干脆把他。  
simply treats him  
当成周公弟弟。  
as the Duke of Zhou's brother.  

召公当面发问。  
Duke Shao asked him directly,  
“四哥。  
"Fourth Brother,  
咱们最近。  
why is it that lately  
怎么越来越说不到一起了？”  
we no longer seem able to speak as one?"  
他也起了疑心。  
He too had begun to suspect something.  
可他又了解周公为人。  
Yet he also knew the Duke of Zhou's character.  
所以既怀疑。  
So he was suspicious  
又困惑。  
and puzzled at once.  
周公回答得很直。  
The Duke of Zhou answered plainly.  
“二位。  
"You two,  
我之所以摄政。  
the only reason I serve as regent  
都是为了祖宗基业。  
is to preserve the work of our ancestors  
和大周天下。  
and the realm of Great Zhou.  
如今二哥早死。  
Our second brother died young.  
成王年纪又小。  
King Cheng is still very young.  
天下也远未太平。  
And the world is far from peaceful.  
所以我只能这样做。  
So I have no choice but to act as I do.  
等成王长大。  
Once King Cheng is grown,  
我自然还政给他。”  
I will naturally return the power to him."  
周公话不多。  
The Duke of Zhou did not say much.  
可已经够了。  
But it was enough.  
因为大家都知道。  
For everyone knew  
他是说话算数的人。  
that he kept his word.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`原因状语从句`、`时间状语从句`、`宾语从句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `I serve as regent only because the king is young and the realm is still unsettled.`。<br>
难点拆解：`only because ...` 点明原因；其中 `the king is young and the realm is still unsettled` 是两个并列原因；后面的 `Once King Cheng is grown, I will ...` 又补了一个时间状语从句，说明他何时还政。<br>
常用短语：
- `keep one's word`：说话算数
- `have no choice but to`：只好……
- `far from`：远非，远远不是
- `sit down for a talk`：坐下来谈谈

有了太公和召公。  
With Jiang Taigong and Duke Shao  
的理解和支持。  
now understanding and backing him,  
周公就更稳了。  
the Duke of Zhou stood on much firmer ground.  
第三招。  
The third move  
叫各个击破。  
was to defeat the enemy piece by piece.  
从种种迹象看。  
From all appearances,  
管叔鲜拉了淮夷。  
Guan Shu Xian had drawn in the Huai Yi  
和徐戎做盟友。  
and the Xu Rong as allies.  
若让他们会师。  
If they had been allowed to combine,  
威胁会很大。  
the threat would have been great.  
于是周公。  
So the Duke of Zhou  
让长子展伯禽。  
sent his eldest son, Zhan Boqin,  
先去鲁国。  
ahead to Lu,  
当然也带走。  
and naturally he took with him  
相当一部分兵力。  
a sizable force.  
这样一来。  
That way,  
管叔和禄父。  
Guan Shu and Lufu  
就被夹在。  
were caught  
周朝和鲁国之间。  
between Zhou and Lu.  
与此同时。  
At the same time,  
鲁国又能切断。  
Lu could sever  
管叔和淮夷。  
the link between Guan Shu  
之间的联系。  
and the Huai Yi.  
若还有需要。  
And if need arose,  
姜太公的齐军。  
Jiang Taigong's Qi forces  
随时能南下。  
could march south at any moment  
增援鲁国。  
to reinforce Lu.  

一切布置妥当后。  
Once everything had been arranged,  
周公就下手了。  
the Duke of Zhou struck.  
以周成王名义。  
In King Cheng's name,  
他亲率大军东征。  
he personally led the Zhou army east.  
与此同时。  
At the same time,  
展伯禽在东线。  
Zhan Boqin  
佯攻淮夷。  
feinted against the Huai Yi on the eastern front  
牵制对方兵力。  
and pinned down their strength.  
历史证明。  
History shows  
周公的战略眼光。  
that the Duke of Zhou's strategic（战略的） vision  
确实很高。  
was indeed extraordinary.  
也证明管叔。  
It also shows  
不过是个没头脑的人。  
that Guan Shu was no great mind.  
周军很快。  
The Zhou army quickly  
打垮了管叔和禄父。  
crushed Guan Shu and Lufu.  
两个首恶。  
The two chief culprits  
随后都被处死。  
were then executed.  
至于被煽动的。  
As for the two younger brothers  
蔡叔度和霍叔处。  
Cai Shu Du and Huo Shu Chu,  
他们只受了。  
they received only  
削爵降爵的处罚。  
the stripping or lowering of rank.  
周公对自家兄弟。  
Toward his own brothers,  
已经算很宽了。  
the Duke of Zhou was already quite lenient（宽大的）.  

铲掉管叔和禄父后。  
After Guan Shu and Lufu were removed,  
周军又和伯禽会师。  
the Zhou forces joined up with Boqin.  
然后放开手脚。  
Then they moved freely  
去打淮夷和徐戎。  
against the Huai Yi and Xu Rong.  
最终。  
In the end,  
他们征服了两方。  
they conquered both groups  
也扩大了疆土。  
and expanded Zhou territory.  
鲁国的地盘。  
Lu's lands  
也因此从山东中部。  
thus stretched from central Shandong  
一路扩到。  
all the way out to  
山东南部。  
southern Shandong,  
安徽东部。  
eastern Anhui,  
以及江苏北部。  
and northern Jiangsu.  

### 忠心耿耿
### Loyal Through and Through

成王刚即位时。  
When King Cheng had only just taken the throne,  
年纪很小。  
he was very young,  
身体也不太好。  
and his health was not especially strong.  
周公剪下指甲。  
The Duke of Zhou cut his nails  
投入黄河。  
and cast them into the Yellow River.  
他向河神祈祷。  
He prayed to the river god:  
“成王还小。  
"King Cheng is still young.  
见识也不够。  
He has not yet seen enough of the world.  
若有什么过错。  
If there is any fault,  
都算在我头上吧。”  
let it all fall on me."  
祈祷完后。  
When the prayer was over,  
周公又把祷文。  
the Duke of Zhou hid the written prayer  
藏在自己府里。  
in his own residence.  

有人会说。  
Some may say  
剪个指甲算什么？  
that throwing away nails means very little.  
可在古人眼里。  
But in ancient eyes,  
头发和指甲。  
hair and nails  
都是父母给的。  
were gifts from one's parents  
不能随便丢弃。  
and not to be cast off lightly.  
后来曹操。  
That is also why, later,  
会有“割发代首”的故事。  
there was the story of Cao Cao cutting his hair in place of his head.  
把指甲扔进黄河。  
To throw one's nails into the Yellow River  
等于是在说。  
was as good as saying,  
若我有罪。  
if I am at fault,  
我愿自己。  
I am willing myself  
跳进河里淹死。  
to leap into the river and drown.  

成王即位第七年。  
In the seventh year of King Cheng's reign,  
他已经十九岁。  
he had reached the age of nineteen.  
按当时说法。  
By the standards of that age,  
十九岁。  
nineteen  
已经算成年。  
counted as adulthood.  
周公眼看。  
Seeing that  
天下基本安定了。  
the realm was largely settled  
成王也长成了。  
and King Cheng had grown up,  
周公就把权力。  
the Duke of Zhou returned the power  
交还给成王。  
to King Cheng  
自己退回臣位。  
and stepped back into the rank of ministers.  

成王正式亲政后。  
After King Cheng formally took power,  
难免有人。  
there were, inevitably,  
在背后说周公坏话。  
people whispering against the Duke of Zhou.  
成王心里。  
In time,  
也起了疑心。  
doubt rose in his mind as well.  
周公一看势头不对。  
The Duke of Zhou saw which way the wind was blowing.  
好在他早有准备。  
Fortunately, he had long been prepared for it.  
既然如此。  
So,  
三十六计。  
of the thirty-six stratagems,  
走为上计。  
the best one was to leave.  
他干脆躲去了楚国。  
He simply withdrew to Chu.  
为什么去楚国？  
Why Chu?  
因为那样。  
Because that way  
既不会连累。  
he would not implicate  
自己的儿子和兄弟。  
his sons or brothers,  
而楚国又太小。  
and Chu was still too small a state  
即便想造反。  
to mount a rebellion  
也造不起来。  
even if anyone wanted one.  

总理不见了。  
The prime minister had disappeared.  
成王就下令。  
So King Cheng ordered  
搜查总理府。  
a search of the prime minister's residence.  
看看能不能。  
Perhaps they might find  
找出一点证据。  
some scrap of evidence  
证明周公想篡位。  
that the Duke of Zhou had planned to seize the throne.  
这一搜。  
And the search did turn up something.  
搜出什么？  
What did it find?  
正是当年。  
It was the very prayer  
那篇黄河祷文。  
he had written years earlier beside the Yellow River.  
有人说。  
Some might ask  
这会不会是。  
whether that was a forgery  
他逃走时伪造的？  
made when he fled.  
可那是三千多年前。  
But that was more than three thousand years ago.  
一份放了六七年的旧文书。  
A document six or seven years old  
哪有那么容易伪造。  
was not so easily forged.  
又有人说。  
Others may ask  
会不会周公。  
whether the Duke of Zhou  
早在六七年前。  
had prepared for this very day  
就算到了今天？  
six or seven years in advance.  
可若真如此。  
But if that had truly been the case,  
那他若要篡位。  
then had he wanted the throne,  
岂不是更容易？  
it would only have been easier for him to take it.  
既然如此。  
If so,  
他又何必还政？  
why would he ever have returned it?  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`虚拟语气`、`条件状语从句`、`反问句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `Had the Duke of Zhou truly wanted the throne, he would never have returned it.`。<br>
难点拆解：这里用了省略 `if` 的虚拟语气倒装，正常语序是 `If the Duke of Zhou had truly wanted ...`；主句 `would never have returned it` 表示与过去事实相反的推测；最后的反问句 `why would he ...` 让语气更有力。<br>
常用短语：
- `take the throne`：夺位
- `turn up`：被找到，出现
- `be prepared for`：对……早有准备
- `withdraw to`：退往，避居

成王一下子。  
In that instant,  
就被打动了。  
King Cheng was deeply moved.  
他明白自己错了。  
He understood that he had been wrong.  
他也感到惭愧。  
And he felt ashamed.  
叔叔不避嫌。  
His uncle had not spared himself suspicion,  
替自己打下。  
yet had built for him  
这样安定的基业。  
such a stable inheritance.  
而自己竟然。  
Yet he himself  
还怀疑叔叔。  
had gone so far as to doubt that uncle  
逼得叔叔出走。  
and drive him away.  
这岂不是罪过？  
Was that not a sin?  
于是成王。  
So King Cheng  
派人去楚国。  
sent men to Chu  
把周公请了回来。  
and invited the Duke of Zhou back.  
叔侄相见。  
When uncle and nephew met again,  
两人相对而泣。  
they wept facing one another.  
周公这才知道。  
Only then did the Duke of Zhou know  
自己的苦心。  
that his painstaking loyalty  
终究没有白费。  
had not, in the end, been wasted.  

周公又担心。  
The Duke of Zhou still worried  
成王将来。  
that King Cheng might one day  
沉迷享乐。  
sink into pleasure and ease.  
于是写下。  
So he wrote  
《多士》和《毋逸》。  
*Many Officers* and *Against Ease*,  
来教他治国。  
to teach him how to govern.  
从那以后。  
From then on,  
周公又替周朝。  
the Duke of Zhou further equipped Zhou  
备下一整套制度。  
with a complete set of institutions,  
把国家理得。  
and the state was ordered  
井井有条。  
with impressive order.  

### 尊崇无限
### Endless Reverence

周公临终之前。  
Before he died,  
留下遗嘱。  
the Duke of Zhou left instructions.  
他说。  
He said,  
“一定把我。  
"Be sure to bury me  
葬在成周。  
in Chengzhou,  
这样才能表明。  
so as to show  
我不敢离开。  
that I do not dare  
成王左右。”  
to leave King Cheng's side."  
可等周公死后。  
But after the Duke of Zhou died,  
成王却把他。  
King Cheng buried him  
葬在文王墓旁。  
beside King Wen,  
也就是毕地。  
at Bi, where the ancestral tombs lay.  
而且给了他。  
And he granted him  
王者级别的待遇。  
honors fit for a king.  
成王这样做。  
By doing this,  
是想表明。  
King Cheng meant to show  
自己不敢。  
that he himself did not dare  
把周公只当臣子。  
to treat the Duke of Zhou as a mere subject.  

周公去世那年秋天。  
In the autumn of the year he died,  
秋收前忽然。  
before the harvest,  
狂风暴雨大作。  
violent wind and rain suddenly swept in.  
地里的麦子。  
All the wheat in the fields  
全被吹倒。  
was flattened.  
树也被连根拔起。  
Trees too were uprooted.  
全国上下。  
The whole Zhou realm  
都惊恐不安。  
was shaken with fear.  
成王赶紧沐浴更衣。  
King Cheng hurried to bathe and dress in proper garments.  
又和召公、毕公。  
Then, together with Duke Shao and Duke Bi,  
赶去祖庙。  
he went to the ancestral temple  
打开金滕匮。  
and opened the metal-bound casket.  
这一下才看到。  
Only then did he discover  
当年那篇祷文。  
the old prayer.  
成王大惊。  
King Cheng was stunned.  
立刻问守庙官。  
He immediately questioned the temple officers.  
这是怎么回事。  
What did this mean?  
守庙的人回答。  
The keepers answered,  
“确有此事。  
"It truly happened.  
只是周公当时。  
The Duke of Zhou simply  
不让我们说。”  
forbade us to speak of it."  
成王听完。  
After hearing this,  
哭了。  
King Cheng wept.  
换了谁。  
Who would not,  
谁能不哭？  
in his place?  

成王说。  
King Cheng said,  
“这是上天。  
"This is Heaven  
在警醒我。  
warning me  
也是在表彰。  
and at the same time honoring  
周公的大德。”  
the Duke of Zhou's great virtue."  
于是他决定。  
So he decided  
以天子之礼。  
to offer a suburban sacrifice to the Duke of Zhou  
郊祭周公。  
with rites fit for the Son of Heaven.  
祭祀之后。  
After the sacrifice,  
乌云散去。  
the clouds broke,  
风也转向。  
the wind turned,  
麦子一片片。  
and the fallen wheat  
又重新站了起来。  
stood back up across the fields.  
那一年。  
That year  
天下大丰收。  
the whole realm had a great harvest.  

这是神话吗？  
Is this a myth?  
作者说。  
The author says no.  
高尚的德行。  
Great virtue  
本就可以感天动地。  
can move Heaven and earth.  
为了表达。  
To show his reverence and gratitude,  
成王后来还特许。  
King Cheng later specially allowed  
鲁国郊祭文王。  
Lu to make suburban sacrifice to King Wen,  
也让鲁侯。  
and let the lords of Lu  
享受天子级的礼乐。  
enjoy rites and music at a royal level.  
周公死后。  
After the Duke of Zhou died,  
他的爵位。  
his title  
由后代世袭。  
passed down hereditarily.  
于是周公一系。  
So in effect, the Duke of Zhou's line  
等于有两套爵位。  
held two hereditary dignities.  
一套在中央。  
One was the central title  
是“周公”。  
of Duke of Zhou.  
一套在地方。  
The other was the feudal rulership  
是“鲁侯”。  
of the lord of Lu.  

世世代代。  
Generation after generation,  
周公的事迹。  
the Duke of Zhou's deeds  
和品德。  
and virtue  
都被反复传颂。  
were praised again and again.  
中国历代宰相。  
Prime ministers across Chinese history  
几乎都爱说。  
have all liked to say  
自己要向周公学习。

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`定语从句`、`情态动词`、`并列结构`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `Only a ruler who loves the people can be called virtuous and receive Heaven's mandate.`。<br>
难点拆解：`Only a ruler ...` 先限定“只有什么样的人”；`who loves the people` 是定语从句，修饰 `ruler`；后面 `can be called ... and receive ...` 是两个并列动作，一个说“算不算有德”，一个说“能不能承受天命”。<br>
常用短语：
- `draw near to`：亲近
- `take responsibility`：承担责任
- `win over`：争取，拉拢
- `Heaven's mandate`：天命

### 鞠躬尽瘁
### Utter Devotion

儿子伯禽。  
When his son Boqin  
要去鲁国做国君时。

周公说过一段。

千古闻名的话。

大意是。  
Their meaning was this:  
我是文王的儿子。  
I am the son of King Wen,  
武王的弟弟。  
the brother of King Wu,  
也是成王的叔叔。  
and the uncle of King Cheng.  
我的身份。  
My rank in the world  
已经不低了。

Even so,

所以我洗头时。

若有贤士上门。  
if a worthy man arrives,  
我会把头发。  
I grasp my wet hair  
一把握住。  
and rise to meet him.  
一顿饭。  
At a single meal,  
若有贤士来见。

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`让步状语从句`、`时间状语从句`、`条件状语从句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `Even so, I still rise from my bath or my meal whenever a worthy man arrives.`。<br>
难点拆解：`Even so` 表示“即便如此”；`whenever a worthy man arrives` 是时间兼条件色彩很强的从句，表示“只要一来，我就立刻去见”；主句里把 `my bath` 和 `my meal` 并列起来，强调他对贤才的重视。<br>
常用短语：
- `worthy man`：贤士
- `grow arrogant`：变得骄傲
- `set aside`：放下，搁开
- `rise to meet`：起身相迎

写下“周公吐哺”。

《说苑》里还记着。  
The *Garden of Anecdotes* also records  
周公每隔一阵。  
that every so often the Duke of Zhou  
会派十二个人。  
would dispatch twelve men.  
三人一组。  
Working in teams of three,  
分往东南西北。  
they went east, south, west, and north.  
干什么？  
What for?  
访求贤人。

体察民间疾苦。  
to look into the people's hardships,  
然后回来。  
and then return  
向他报告。  
to report to him.  
等诸侯来朝时。

周公又会把。  
the Duke of Zhou would tell them  
他们本国的得失。  
the strengths and failings of their own states,  
以及百姓好恶。

诸侯听了。

既震惊又害怕。  
and more than a little afraid.  
百姓听说了。

就会高兴。  
they were glad

and took hope.  
于是诸侯。

去施行德政。

while the people  
安居乐业。

《诗经》里。

也因此有句子。

“柔远能迩。

## 第二十三章 阿哥阿妹情义长
## Chapter 23: A Brother and Sister Bound Too Deep

说完周公。  
Now that the Duke of Zhou is done,  
再说姜太公。  
let us turn to Jiang Taigong.  
姜太公的故事很多。  
Stories about Jiang Taigong are countless.  
像什么姜太公钓鱼。  
Tales like Jiang Taigong fishing  
这里就不展开了。  
can wait for another time.  
但有一点毫无疑问。  
One thing, however, is beyond doubt:  
在周灭商这件事上。  
in the overthrow of Shang,  
老爷子功劳极大。  
the old man deserved immense credit.  

有段对话。  
There is one exchange in particular  
很值得一提。  
that deserves attention.  
周文王问他。  
King Wen asked him  
该怎样治理国家。  
how a state ought to be governed.  
姜太公回答说。  
Jiang Taigong replied,  
“能称王的国家。  
"A state fit to rule  
百姓要富足。  
has prosperous people.  
能称霸的国家。  
A state fit to dominate  
军人要富足。  
has prosperous soldiers.  
混日子的国家。  
A state that merely muddles along  
官员会富足。  
has prosperous officials.  
快完蛋的国家。  
A state on the verge of ruin  
则是国君富。  
has a rich ruler  
而百姓穷。  
and poor people.  
所以。  
So  
国富民穷。  
if the ruler is rich while the people are poor,  
是很危险的。”  
the state is in real danger."  
文王听完后。  
When King Wen heard this,  
立刻把国库里的财物。  
he immediately took wealth from the royal treasury  
分给了百姓。  
and distributed it among the people.  
作者连声感叹。  
The author cannot help exclaiming  
姜太公真伟大。  
how great Jiang Taigong was,  
这判断也真伟大。  
and how great that judgment was.  
三千年后。  
Three thousand years later,  
“增加老百姓财产性收入”  
calls to increase people's property income  
才又被正式提起。  
would be formally heard again.  
说到底。  
At bottom,  
国富民穷。  
a rich state with poor people  
确实危险。  
is indeed dangerous.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`定语后置`、`并列句`、`条件色彩的判断句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If the ruler is rich while the people are poor, the state is in real danger.`。<br>
难点拆解：前半句用 `while` 把“君富”和“民穷”并列对照出来；整句虽然没有直写 `if`，但逻辑上带有很强的条件色彩；后半句 `the state is in real danger` 是直接判断。<br>
常用短语：
- `on the verge of ruin`：濒临覆亡
- `muddle along`：勉强混日子
- `distribute ... among ...`：把……分给……
- `at bottom`：归根到底

灭商以后。  
After Shang fell,  
武王把姜太公。  
King Wu enfeoffed（分封） Jiang Taigong  
封到了齐国。  
in Qi.  
齐国都城在营丘。  
Its capital was Yingqiu,  
也就是今天淄博一带。  
around present-day Zibo.  
姜太公治理齐国。  
When Jiang Taigong governed Qi,  
并没有硬改风俗。  
he did not force local customs to change.  
他只是简化旧礼。  
He merely simplified old rites,  
发展工商业。  
developed industry and trade,  
鼓励养鱼贩盐。  
and encouraged fishing and the salt trade.  
结果周边百姓。  
As a result, people from surrounding states  
纷纷往齐国跑。  
flocked to Qi.  
姜太公只用三年。  
Jiang Taigong took only three years  
就把齐国治理好了。  
to put Qi in order.  
而南边的鲁侯伯禽。  
By contrast, Boqin in Lu to the south  
花了五年。  
spent five years  
去改造当地风俗。  
reforming local customs and ritual.

把这个难题处理掉。  
solve this awkward problem.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`反问句`、`条件状语从句`、`并列谓语`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If Qi had so many beauties, why would Zhuer risk the shame of incest just to pursue Wen Jiang?`。<br>
难点拆解：前半句 `If Qi had so many beauties` 是条件状语从句；后半句是反问句，用来加强说服力；`risk ...` 和 `pursue ...` 形成很强的动作逻辑。<br>
常用短语：
- `make up one's mind`：下定决心
- `risk the shame of`：冒着……的骂名
- `think it over`：仔细想想
- `solve an awkward problem`：解决棘手问题

姑奶奶嫁的人。

比你还体面。

正百感交集时。

诸儿派人。

Prince Hui first went to see  
齐僖公夫人。  
the duchess of Qi,  
把礼物献上。  
offered the gifts,  
并说道。  
and said,  
“奉我国君之命。  
"By the order of my lord,  
前来献上薄礼。  
I present these humble gifts  
迎娶夫人的贤女。”  
to receive Your Grace's worthy daughter."  
夫人答道。  
The duchess replied,  
“小女从小娇养。  
"My daughter has been delicately raised  
不太懂事。  
and knows too little of the world.  
如今有幸。  
That she may now serve  
侍奉贵国国君。  
the lord of your honored state  
怎能不感激？”  
is more honor than I can thank you for."  
她接过礼物。  
She accepted the gifts,  
把其中一双鞋。  
took one pair of shoes,  
给女儿穿上。  
and put them on her daughter.  
又给她梳妆打扮。  
Then she dressed and adorned her,  
并叮嘱道。  
and instructed her:  
“去吧。  
"Go now.  
从今以后。  
From this day on,  
要孝顺公婆。  
be filial to your parents-in-law,  
让家里和睦。  
keep the household harmonious,  
别总惦记着。  
and do not spend your days  
往娘家跑。”  
thinking of running back to your mother's home."  
随后文姜。  
After that, Wen Jiang  
先拜别母亲。  
took leave of her mother,  
再到厅上。  
then went to the hall  
拜别父亲。  
to bid farewell to her father,  
到了门口。  
and at the gate  
又和众亲戚告别。  
she said goodbye to the relatives.  
之后她登上。  
She then boarded a Qi carriage  
齐国的车。  
and,  
在公子翚和齐国下卿。  
under the escort of Prince Hui  
一同护送下。  
and a lower minister of Qi,  
前往鲁国。  
set out for Lu.  
到了瓘邑。  
At Guan,  
再完成迎亲礼。  
the reception rites were performed,  
她改乘鲁桓公的车。  
she transferred to Duke Huan's carriage,  
一路去了曲阜。  
and traveled on to Qufu,  
然后举行婚礼。  
where the wedding was held.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`并列句`、`被动色彩结构`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If the groom was the heir, he went straight to the bride's capital, but if he was the ruler himself, he was not supposed to cross his own borders.`。<br>
难点拆解：前后各有一个 `if` 条件状语从句；中间用 `but` 做对照；`was not supposed to` 表示“按礼制本不该”。<br>
常用短语：
- `heir apparent`：世子
- `be supposed to`：按理应当
- `take leave of`：辞别
- `transfer to`：转乘

### 送亲失礼
### A Breach of Ritual

可这次迎亲。  
Yet this wedding escort  
还是被齐僖公。  
was partly spoiled  
坏了规矩。  
by Duke Xi of Qi himself.  
鲁桓公三年。  
In Duke Huan's third year,  
也就是前709年。  
that is, 709 BC,  
鲁桓公迎娶文姜。  
he married Wen Jiang.  
照《左传》说。  
According to the *Zuo Commentary*,  
若是地位相当的诸侯。  
when two lords of equal rank arranged a marriage,  
嫁的是姐妹。  
an elder minister escorted a sister,  
嫁的是女儿。  
while a lower minister escorted a daughter.  
若小国国君之女。  
If the daughter of a small state's ruler  
嫁给大国国君或世子。  
married the ruler or heir of a great state,  
那就要上卿去送。  
then a high minister had to escort her.  
若诸侯之女。  
If a feudal lord's daughter  
嫁给周王或王太子。  
married the Zhou king or crown prince,  
那更是。  
then all the great officers  
群卿同送。  
went together.  
但无论哪一种。  
But in any case,  
国君本人。  
the ruler himself  
都不该亲送。  
was never meant to escort her in person.  
照这个规矩。  
By that rule,  
齐国这次。  
Qi this time  
只该派下卿。  
should have sent only a lower minister.  
可齐僖公。  
But Duke Xi  
实在舍不得女儿。  
could not bear parting with his daughter.  
于是决定。  
So he decided  
亲自去送。  
to escort her himself.  
当然。  
To be fair,  
他没送到曲阜。  
he did not go all the way to Qufu.  
只送到了瓘邑。  
He stopped at Guan.  
《左传》评论得很直。  
The *Zuo Commentary* judged the matter bluntly:  
“齐侯送姜氏。  
"The lord of Qi escorted Lady Jiang.  
非礼也。”  
It was against ritual."  
由此也能看出。  
From this alone one can see  
鲁国人讲究礼。  
that Lu people cared keenly for ritual,  
齐国人则随性得多。  
while Qi people were much more easygoing.  

### 兄妹缠绵
### Brother and Sister Entwined

回到曲阜。  
Back in Qufu,  
婚礼一完。  
once the wedding ended,  
就是洞房花烛夜。  
it was time for the wedding night.  
那一夜。  
That night  
文姜给了鲁桓公。  
Wen Jiang gave Duke Huan  
一个大惊喜。

她还是处女。  
she was still a virgin.  
那年代可没人。  
In that age there was no trickery  
去造什么处女之身。  
to fake such a thing.  
文姜是真的。  
Wen Jiang truly  
保住了清白。  
had preserved her maidenhood.  
鲁桓公顿时。  
Duke Huan at once  
高兴坏了。

在他看来。  
In his eyes,  
这简直赚大了。  
he had made the bargain of a lifetime.  
从那以后。  
From then on,  
他对文姜。  
he doted on Wen Jiang  
百般宠爱。  
in every possible way  
几乎言听计从。  
and almost did whatever she asked.  

三年后。  
Three years later,  
文姜生了个儿子。  
Wen Jiang bore a son.  
巧得很。  
By a neat coincidence,  
孩子生日。  
the child's birthday  
和桓公是同一天。  
fell on the same day as Duke Huan's.  
于是起名姬同。  
So he was named Ji Tong,  
并被立为世子。  
and made heir.  
又过了八年。  
Another eight years passed.  
齐僖公死了。  
Duke Xi of Qi died.  
诸儿登基。  
Zhuer took the throne  
成了齐襄公。  
and became Duke Xiang of Qi.  
如今他成了齐国老大。  
Now that he was master of Qi,  
再也没人管得住他。  
there was no one left to restrain him.  
于是他放开了玩。  
So he plunged more freely into his pleasures.  
又过四年。  
Four more years passed.  
什么都玩腻了。  
He had grown tired of everything.  
偏在这时。  
And at just that moment,  
他忽然又想起。  
he remembered  
妹妹文姜。  
his sister Wen Jiang.  
他反复低吟。  
He murmured again and again,  
“今兹不折。  
"If not plucked this spring,  
讵无来春？  
is there no spring to come?  
叮咛兮。  
Remember,  
复叮咛！”  
remember again!"  
十五个春天。  
Fifteen springs  
一晃过去。  
had slipped by.  
那朵桃花。  
Could that peach blossom  
还能不能再开？  
still bloom again?  

这时齐国送来国书。  
Then an official invitation arrived from Qi.  
齐襄公盛情邀请。

鲁桓公去齐国。  
Duke Huan of Lu  
做一次国事访问。  
for a state visit.  
信里还特意写着。

“请携夫人同来。”  
"Please bring your lady wife as well."  
鲁桓公决定。  
Duke Huan decided  
接受邀请。  
to accept  
并带文姜同行。  
and bring Wen Jiang with him.  
大夫申需劝他。  
The officer Shen Xu tried to dissuade him.  
他说。  
He said,  
“女子出嫁后。  
"Once a woman has married away,  
若父母还在。

回去看看也罢。

我看。

鲁桓公却不听。

他心里想。

别瞎担心。

Besides,  
文姜嫁给我时。  
when Wen Jiang married me,  
还是处女呢。

可这回。  
But this time  
鲁桓公错了。

他只知道。

却不知道。

有些地方。  
that some places  
一脚下去。

十五年过去。  
Fifteen years had passed.  
文姜越来越想。  
Wen Jiang missed Zhuer  
念着诸儿。  
more and more.  
不错。  
True,  
鲁桓公是个好人。  
Duke Huan was a good man.  
可他太书生气。

不会讲黄段子。

不会玩花样。

也不懂浪漫。

文姜觉得。  
Wen Jiang felt  
这样的日子。  
that such a life  
没有滋味。

在鲁国。  
In Lu,  
她远不如。

鲁桓公十八年。  
In Duke Huan's eighteenth year,  
也就是前694年。  
that is, 694 BC,  
夫妇二人到了齐国。

齐襄公亲自出迎。

他一见文姜。

心里就翻了江。

十五年过去。

依然那么迷人。

桃花既然还开着。

欢迎很热烈。

鲁桓公没提防。

爱一个人。

就该信一个人。

晚宴结束后。  
When the banquet ended,  
鲁桓公喝得有些多。  
Duke Huan had drunk rather a lot.  
便准备回宾馆睡觉。  
So he prepared to return to his lodge and sleep.  
文姜这时提出。  
At that moment Wen Jiang said,  
“我十五年没回来了。  
"I have not come back for fifteen years.  
想去宫里。  
I want to go into the palace,

see the rooms of my girlhood,  
也好怀念爹娘。  
and remember my father and mother.  
行吗？”  
May I?"  
这话很堂皇。

鲁桓公爽快答应。

“去吧。  
"Go on.  
早点回来。”  
Come back early."  
于是文姜。  
So Wen Jiang  
去了齐宫后宫。

闺房自然没有。

倒是有人。  
But someone had  
早替她备好。

一间洞房。

里面烛光昏暗。

空无一人。

文姜轻轻进去。  
Wen Jiang stepped in softly,  
轻轻关门。  
closed the door softly,  
也轻轻吟道。  
and softly recited,  
“桃有华。  
"The peach has blossom,  
灿灿其霞。  
bright as rosy clouds.  
当户不折。  
Unplucked before my door,  
飘而为苴。  
it drifted away like weed.  
吁嗟兮。  
Alas, alas,  
复吁嗟！”  
alas again!"  
昏暗角落里。  
From the dark corner  
走出一个人。  
stepped a figure.  
他也接着念。

“桃有英。  
"The peach has glory,  
烨烨其灵。  
bright with living light.  
今兹不折。  
If not plucked this spring,  
讵无来春？  
is there no spring to come?  
叮咛兮。  
Remember,  
复叮咛！”  
remember again!"  
“诸儿哥哥。”  
"Brother Zhuer,"  
文姜轻轻唤他。  
Wen Jiang called softly.  
“文儿妹妹。”  
"Sister Wen'er,"  
Zhuer answered,  
满脸都是淫笑。  
his face already full of hungry delight.  
两人抱在一起。  
They embraced each other  
像分离十五年的情人。  
like lovers parted for fifteen years.  
“妹妹。  
"Sister,  
你终于来了。  
you have finally come.  
我的春天回来了。”  
My spring has returned."  
“哥哥。  
"Brother,  
我好想你。

想你的黄段子。  
I missed your dirty jokes,  
也想你身上的味道。”  
and even the smell of you."  
“那我讲给你听。”  
"Then let me tell them to you again."  
十五年的相思。  
Fifteen years of longing,  
十五年的压抑。  
fifteen years of repression,  
像干柴遇见烈火。

那一刻。

伦理被扔开。  
ethics were cast aside,  
欲望被点燃。  
and desire was lit.  
那一晚。  
That night

left them utterly undone.

that time passed too quickly.  

### 鲁桓公之死
### The Death of Duke Huan of Lu

鲁桓公回到住处。  
Duke Huan returned to his lodge.  
先用冷水。  
He splashed cold water  
洗了把脸。  
over his face  
人也清醒一些。  
and cleared his head a little.  
他躺在床上。  
He lay on the bed,  
却睡不着。  
but could not sleep.  
他一直等文姜。  
He kept waiting for Wen Jiang.  
左等不来。  
She did not come when he waited once,  
右等也不来。  
nor when he waited again.  
等着等着。  
At last, waiting on and on,  
他自己先睡着了。  
he fell asleep first.  
半夜里。  
In the middle of the night,  
他渴得厉害。  
he woke terribly thirsty.  
起身想要水喝。  
He sat up wanting water,  
却发现文姜。  
only to find that Wen Jiang  
竟还没回来。  
still had not returned.  
这下他有点慌。  
Now he began to worry.  
难道真出事了？  
Could something truly be wrong?  
他又等。  
He waited again.  
等到天亮。  
By dawn,  
文姜依旧不见。  
Wen Jiang was still nowhere in sight.  
他困极了。  
Exhausted,  
又昏沉睡去。  
he sank back into a heavy sleep.  

太阳升起时。  
When the sun finally rose,  
文姜回来了。  
Wen Jiang returned.  
她头发有点乱。  
Her hair was somewhat disordered.  
脸色也有点倦。  
Her face looked tired.  
眼圈微黑。  
There were shadows under her eyes.  
进门时。  
As she came in,  
她还打了个呵欠。  
she even yawned.  
鲁桓公压着怒火问。  
Duke Huan asked, holding down his anger,  
“夫人。  
"Madam,  
你怎么夜里。  
how is it  
一夜不归？”  
that you did not return all night?"  
文姜答得很轻。  
Wen Jiang answered lightly,  
“嗨，熟人太多。  
"Oh, too many familiar faces.  
又喝了点酒。”  
I drank a bit too much."  
鲁桓公追问。  
Duke Huan pressed on.  
“那也该回来。  
"You still should have come back.  
昨晚你和谁在一起？”  
Who were you with last night?"  
“我嫂子连妃。  
"My sister-in-law, Lady Lian.  
十几年不见。  
It had been over ten years.  
所以叙了叙旧。”  
We were catching up."  
鲁桓公一下怒了。  
Duke Huan flared at once.  
“胡说。  
"Nonsense.  
你嫁给我时。  
When you married me,  
连妃还没进宫。  
Lady Lian was not even in the palace yet.  
你们叙什么旧？”  
What old times were you catching up on?"  
这一下。  
At that,  
文姜不说话了。  
Wen Jiang fell silent.  
她一直以为。  
She had always thought  
鲁桓公很好骗。  
Duke Huan was easy to fool.  
谁知道。  
Who knew  
他也没那么好骗。  
he was not so simple after all.  
鲁桓公咬牙追问。  
Grinding his teeth, Duke Huan demanded,  
“说。  
"Speak.  
是不是和你哥哥。  
Were you with your brother?"  
“没，没有。”  
"N-no."  
“哼。  
"Hmph.  
当初你名声臭了街。  
Your name once stank through the streets.  
我不计前嫌。  
I ignored all that,  
一心一意娶了你。  
married you wholeheartedly,  
也一直待你好。  
and treated you with all sincerity.  
谁知你不思悔改。  
Who knew you would not repent,  
反而变本加厉。  
but only grow worse?  
你对得起我吗？  
How can you face me?  
和自己的亲哥哥上床。  
To go to bed with your own brother,  
你简直禽兽不如。  
you are worse than a beast.  
今天咱们就走。  
We leave today.  
回到鲁国。  
Back in Lu,  
我再跟你算账。”  
I will settle accounts with you."  
文姜这下哭了。  
At this, Wen Jiang burst into tears.  
里面一半是后悔。  
Half of it was regret,  
一半是害怕。  
and half was fear.  

齐襄公很快。  
Before long, Duke Xiang  
又派人来请鲁桓公。  
sent someone to invite Duke Huan again.  
意思是。  
The message was simple.  
若要走。  
If he meant to leave,  
也别走得太匆忙。

总得让大舅哥。

尽一点地主之谊。

说得更直白些。  
More plainly:  
请鲁桓公。  
Duke Huan was invited  
去郊外别墅。  
to an out-of-town villa  
吃点野味。  
for game dishes  
顺便践行。  
and a farewell feast.  
盛情难却。  
Such warmth was hard to refuse.  
更何况。  
Besides,  
这里还是齐国地盘。  
this was still Qi's territory.  
真闹翻了。  
If things truly turned ugly,  
不会有好果子吃。  
nothing good would come of it.  
鲁桓公只好。  
So Duke Huan could only  
硬着头皮去了。  
go with forced courage.  
齐襄公待他。  
Duke Xiang received him warmly,  
热情得很。  
as though the woman he had slept with the night before  
就像昨晚和他上床的。  
had not been  
不是鲁桓公的夫人。  
Duke Huan's wife at all.  
席上齐襄公。  
At table Duke Xiang  
一边猛劝酒。  
kept pressing wine on him,  
一边讲黄段子。  
and kept telling dirty jokes  
想逗他高兴。  
to cheer him up.  
鲁桓公对黄段子。  
Duke Huan had no taste  
当然没兴趣。  
for dirty jokes.

## 第二十四章 甜瓜命案

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`原因状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`结果状语从句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `Since our lord was killed by Peng Sheng, we should demand that the Duke of Qi execute him, so that both sides can step down.`。<br>
难点拆解：`Since our lord was killed ...` 是原因状语从句；`that the Duke of Qi execute him` 是宾语从句；`so that both sides can step down` 表示结果和目的，说明这个方案为什么“好用”。<br>
常用短语：
- `carry the death notice`：报丧
- `bear the light`：见得了光
- `have a way to step down`：有台阶下
- `way out`：解决办法

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`并列句`、`将来意味`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If you insist on forcing me back, I will dash my head and die.`。<br>
难点拆解：`If you insist on ...` 是条件状语从句；后面的 `I will ...` 直接给出后果；前文 `I do not wish to ... nor to ...` 又用并列结构把她的态度说得很死。<br>
常用短语：
- `make up one's mind`：下定决心
- `live on the border`：住在边界上
- `insist on`：坚持要……
- `dash one's head and die`：一头撞死

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`虚拟语气`、`时间状语从句`、`结果句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `Had Duke Xiang kept his word, the Sweet Melon Plan would never have been born.`。<br>
难点拆解：这里用了省略 `if` 的虚拟语气倒装，正常语序是 `If Duke Xiang had kept his word ...`；主句 `would never have been born` 表示与过去事实相反的结果。<br>
常用短语：
- `change one's tone`：翻脸，变口风
- `count on one's fingers`：掰着指头算
- `draw up a plan`：拟定计划
- `keep one's word`：守信

### 管鲍之交

管仲。

字夷吾。

西周的宗法制度里。  
Under the Western Zhou clan hierarchy（宗法制度）,

the Son of Heaven stood as the great ancestral line of all under Heaven.

Guan Zhong invested less.  
鲍叔牙出钱多。  
Bao Shuya put in more.  
可分红的时候。  
Yet when the profits were divided,  
管仲拿得更多。  
Guan Zhong took the larger share,  
鲍叔牙反而拿得少。  
while Bao Shuya took less.  
旁人看不过去。  
Onlookers could not believe it.  
你出钱多。  
"You paid more.  
怎么分得反而少？  
How did you end up with less?"  
他们问鲍叔牙。  
they asked Bao Shuya.  
鲍叔牙只说。  
Bao Shuya merely replied,  
管仲家里穷。  
"Guan Zhong's family is poorer.  
他比我更需要钱。  
He needs the money more than I do."  
有人冷笑。  
Someone sneered,  
小心哪天。  
"Be careful. One day  
他把你卖了。  
he'll sell you out,  
你还替他数钱。  
and you'll still be counting the coins for him."  
鲍叔牙却不改口。  
Bao Shuya did not change his tune.  
他不会。  
"He wouldn't."  
于是又有人。  
So others went to Guan Zhong  
跑去问管仲。  
and questioned him too.  
你出钱少。  
"You invested less.  
怎么反而拿得多？  
How can you take more?"  
管仲的回答也很直接。  
Guan Zhong answered just as plainly.  
鲍叔牙家比我富。  
"Bao Shuya's family is richer than mine.  
我更需要钱。  
I need it more."  
别人骂他不厚道。  
People called him unfair.  
还问以后谁肯。  
They asked who would ever  
再跟他合伙。  
want to partner with him again.  
管仲说。  
Guan Zhong said,  
鲍叔牙啊。  
"Bao Shuya would."  

这门生意。  
That business, however,  
开头还算不错。  
began well enough  
后来却越做越差。  
and then steadily worsened.  
到最后。  
In the end,  
连本钱都差点赔光。  
they nearly lost their capital（本钱）.  
只能收手。  
They had to give it up.  
于是又有人说。  
Again people said,  
管仲只会吹牛。  
"Guan Zhong is all talk  
真干事不行。  
and no good at real work."  
鲍叔牙还是替他解释。  
Bao Shuya still defended him.  
别这么说。  
"Don't say that.  
他不是骗子。  
He isn't a fraud（骗子）,  
也不是蠢。  
and he certainly isn't foolish.  
只是时运不好。  
He has simply had bad luck.  
生意没做成。  
The business failed  
不是因为他没本事。  
not because he lacks ability."  

可有意思的是。  
Yet the interesting part is this.  
鲍叔牙自己单干。  
When Bao Shuya later worked on his own,  
生意却做得很红火。  
his business flourished（兴旺）.  
他做什么？  
What line was he in?  
开饭店。  
He ran a restaurant.  
而且生意极好。  
And it did extremely well.  
他的店。  
His establishment（店铺）  
以诚信出名。  
was famous for honesty.  
绝不缺斤少两。  
He never cheated on weight or measure.  
也不用坏油。  
He never used rancid（变质的） oil,  
更不会拿病死猪充数。  
and he certainly did not pass off diseased pork as good meat.  
店里总是。  
The place was always  
收拾得干干净净。  
spotlessly clean.  
去他那里吃饭。  
Eating there  
让人十分放心。  
made people feel perfectly safe.  
还有一点。  
There was another reason too.  
他家菜做得特别好。  
The food was exceptionally good.  
人人都赞不绝口。  
Everyone praised it.  
原因也不难猜。  
The reason is easy enough to guess.  
店里有位名厨。  
There was a brilliant chef in the kitchen.  
他叫雍巫。  
His name was Yong Wu.  
字易牙。  
His courtesy name was Yiya.  
后人就都叫他易牙。  
Later generations remembered him simply as Yiya.  
易牙做菜。  
Yiya's cooking  
色香味俱全。  
had color, aroma（香气）, and flavor in perfect balance,  
还总能翻出新花样。  
and he was always inventing something new.  
后来。  
Later on,  
鲍叔牙被尊为。  
Bao Shuya was honored  
饭店业的祖师爷。  
as a founding master of the restaurant trade,  
易牙则成了。  
while Yiya became  
厨师行当的祖师爷。  
a patron figure for cooks.  
说到易牙的厨艺。  
As for Yiya's culinary（烹饪的） skill,  
孔子和孟子。  
both Confucius and Mencius  
都给过极高评价。  
spoke of it very highly.  
《列子》里说。  
The *Liezi* says  
孔子曾感叹。  
that Confucius once remarked  
就算把两种河水。  
that even if two rivers  
混在一起。  
were mixed together,  
易牙一尝。  
Yiya could taste them once  
也能分出来。  
and tell them apart.  
孟子也说过。  
Mencius likewise said  
论到味道。  
that when it came to flavor,  
天下人都认易牙第一。  
everyone under Heaven looked to Yiya as the standard.  
《荀子》也把他。  
The *Xunzi* too  
当成谈论美味的代表。  
used him as the emblem（代表） of perfect taste.  
总之。  
In short,  
讲美食。  
if you spoke of fine food,  
绕不开易牙。  
you could not avoid Yiya.  
后来人把。  
Later generations even turned  
吃顿好的。  
the idea of enjoying a proper feast  
叫作易牙祭。  
into the phrase "an offering to Yiya."  
时间久了。  
Over time,  
这话又慢慢变成。  
the phrase gradually evolved（演变） into  
今天常说的打牙祭。  
what we now call *da yaji*, a rare good meal.  

### 管跑跑

生意不好做。  
Business was unreliable.  
管仲只好。  
So Guan Zhong had to  
想办法混进官场。  
find a way into government service.  
毕竟人总要活。  
A man has to live,  
家里还有老母亲要养。  
and he still had an old mother to support.  
可公务员这条路。  
But the civil-service path  
也没那么好走。  
was no easier.  
管仲前后三次做官。  
Guan Zhong took office three separate times,  
又三次都被炒了。  
and was dismissed all three times.  
很多人因此嘲笑他。  
Many mocked him for it.  
说他不是品行差。  
They said either his character was flawed  
就是个混日子的混混。  
or he was just a drifter（混混） wasting everyone's time.  
鲍叔牙照旧替他说话。  
Bao Shuya, as always, spoke up for him.  
不是他不行。  
"It is not that he is incapable.  
只是他的上司。  
His superiors  
根本不会用他。  
simply do not know how to use him."  

按当时的身份。  
Given their status,  
管仲和鲍叔牙。  
both Guan Zhong and Bao Shuya  
都躲不过兵役。  
had to perform military service.  
鲍叔牙每次打仗。  
In battle, Bao Shuya  
总是冲在前面。  
always charged to the front.  
撤退时。  
When retreat came,  
又常常留在最后。  
he was often the last to leave,  
替大家断后。  
covering everyone else's withdrawal.  
管仲却完全相反。  
Guan Zhong was the exact opposite.  
冲锋的时候。  
When it was time to advance,  
他总落在最后。  
he was always at the rear.  
不是马不肯跑。  
Either the horse would not run,  
就是车轮陷坑。  
or the chariot wheel would sink into a rut.  
总之。  
One way or another,  
他总能掉到最后。  
he always found himself at the back.  
可一旦败了。  
But once the army lost,  
轮到逃命时。  
and it was time to flee,  
他又总跑在最前面。  
he was somehow the first one out.  
这时马也不偷懒了。  
Then the horse stopped dragging its feet,  
车也不掉坑了。  
and the chariot no longer hit any pits.  
三次作战。  
In three campaigns,  
他就逃了三次。  
he ran away three times.  
于是大家都笑他。  
So everyone laughed at him.  
胆小鬼。  
"Coward.  
逃兵。  
Deserter（逃兵）."  
鲍叔牙却每次都解释。  
Yet each time, Bao Shuya explained,  
你们都错了。  
"You are all wrong.  
管仲不是胆小。  
Guan Zhong is not timid（胆小的）,  
也不是逃兵。  
nor is he a deserter.  
他家里有老母亲。  
He has an old mother at home.  
还等着他养。  
She still depends on him.  
他若死了。  
If he dies,  
就没人养她了。  
no one will support her.  
所以他不是怕死。  
So he is not afraid of death.  
他是孝顺。  
He is being filial（孝顺的）."  
不管别人信不信。  
Whether others believed it or not,  
管仲从此。  
from then on, Guan Zhong  
就多了个外号。  
acquired a nickname:  
管跑跑。  
"Guan the Runner."  

两个人在一起时。  
When the two friends discussed things together,  
意见常常不一样。  
their opinions often differed.  
可每到这时。  
But whenever that happened,  
鲍叔牙总会让步。  
Bao Shuya would always yield  
照管仲的意思来。  
and follow Guan Zhong's judgment.  
说白了。  
To put it bluntly,  
管仲做什么。  
whatever Guan Zhong did,  
鲍叔牙都觉得有道理。  
Bao Shuya believed there was sense in it.  
管仲说什么。  
Whatever Guan Zhong said,  
鲍叔牙也都相信。  
Bao Shuya believed that too.  
简直成了。  
It was practically  
两个凡是。  
a doctrine（信条） of "two whatevers."  
管仲后来评价他说。  
Guan Zhong later summed him up like this.  
生我者父母。  
"My parents gave me life,  
知我者鲍叔牙也。  
but Bao Shuya is the one who truly knew me."  
这话既夸了鲍叔牙。  
The remark praised Bao Shuya,  
其实也顺手夸了自己。  
while quietly flattering himself as well.  
鲍叔牙则说。  
Bao Shuya answered,  
能被管仲当成知己。  
"To be counted by Guan Zhong  
做知心朋友。  
as a true friend who understands him  
我已经很高兴了。  
is more than enough to make me happy."  
说到底。  
In the end,  
鲍叔牙就是管仲。  
Bao Shuya was basically  
最铁的头号粉丝。  
Guan Zhong's most loyal admirer.  

### 分工合作

管仲不是小富即安的人。  
Guan Zhong was never the sort of man content（满足的） with small comforts.  
靠做小买卖挣钱。  
Making a little money from petty trade  
不是他的目标。  
was not his goal.  
拿着长矛去冲锋。  
Nor did he want to spend his life  
也不是他的志向。  
charging into battle with a spear.  
他真正想要的。  
What he truly wanted  
是坐进总理办公室。  
was to sit in the prime minister's office  
指挥别人打仗。  
and direct the fighting from there.  
有一天闲聊时。  
One day, while chatting,  
鲍叔牙问他。  
Bao Shuya asked him,  
你说我以后。  
"Tell me,  
将来能做到什么位置？  
how far do you think I can go?"  
管仲看得很直。  
Guan Zhong answered bluntly.  
你这人正直。  
"You are upright  
也有原则。  
and principled.  
可你太正直。  
But you are too upright  
也太有原则。  
and too principled.  
不懂得变通。  
You do not know how to bend.  
做到亚卿。  
At best, you will rise  
大概也就到头了。  
to the rank of vice-minister and stop there."  
鲍叔牙又问。  
Bao Shuya then asked,  
那你呢？  
"And what about you?"  
管仲一点也不客气。  
Guan Zhong showed no false modesty.  
上卿。  
"Top minister.  
我要是当不上上卿。  
If I do not become top minister,  
那是齐国的损失。  
that will be Qi's loss.  
说得再直白一点。  
To be even more direct,  
齐国若只想治好国内。  
if Qi only wants orderly government,  
有你就够了。  
you are enough.  
可若想称霸天下。  
But if it wants to dominate（称霸） the realm,  
那就得靠我。  
then it will need me."  
他说这话时。  
When he said this,  
脸上没有半点得意。  
there was not the slightest smugness（自满） on his face.  
倒像是在陈述事实。  
He spoke as if stating a plain fact.  
鲍叔牙只说。  
Bao Shuya simply said,  
知道了。  
"I understand."  
然后把这话。  
And he engraved（牢记） those words  
牢牢记在心里。  
deep in his heart.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `if Qi only wants orderly government, you are enough, but if it wants to dominate the realm, then it will need me`。<br>
难点拆解：这句话用了两个并列的 `if` 条件结构，前后形成鲜明对比；`you are enough` 说明“治理国家你足够”，`it will need me` 则把“称霸天下”的结果落到主句上。<br>
常用短语：
- `be enough`：足够、胜任
- `dominate the realm`：称霸天下

齐襄公乱伦的事情。  
Duke Xiang's scandalous incest（乱伦）  
早已传遍天下。  
had long spread across the realm.  
别人都在抱怨。  
While others complained  
或拿来当笑话。  
or laughed about it,  
管仲却看到了机会。  
Guan Zhong saw an opportunity.  
他对鲍叔牙说。  
He said to Bao Shuya,  
照襄公这个样子。  
"At Duke Xiang's current pace,  
他蹦跶不了几年了。  
he will not stay on his feet for many more years.  
依我看。  
As I see it,  
他这辈子多半不会有儿子。  
he will probably never have a son.  
那将来能继位的。  
That means the possible heirs  
无非就是公子纠。  
will most likely be Prince Jiu  
或者公子小白。  
or Prince Xiaobai.  
这两个公子。  
At present, neither prince  
现在都没人理会。  
has anyone looking after him.  
咱们正好趁这个空子。  
This is exactly our chance to step in.  
你辅佐一个。  
You support one,  
我辅佐一个。  
and I support the other.  
以后只要有一个。  
As long as one of them  
能当上国君。  
eventually becomes ruler,  
我们就能互相举荐。  
we can recommend each other,  
这才是双保险。  
and that will be true insurance（双保险）."  
鲍叔牙照旧。  
Bao Shuya, as usual,  
满口答应。  
agreed at once.  
于是两人分开。  
So the two divided the work.  
管仲选了公子纠。  
Guan Zhong chose Prince Jiu.  
因为公子纠。  
Prince Jiu  
是哥哥。  
was the elder brother  
脾气也更温和。  
and had a gentler temperament（性情）.  
看上去胜算更大。  
His chances looked much better.  
鲍叔牙心里。  
Bao Shuya felt  
其实并不乐意。  
less than enthusiastic.  
他觉得去辅佐小白。  
Supporting Xiaobai  
没什么前途。  
did not seem promising at all.  
管仲便劝他。  
Guan Zhong persuaded him.  
兄弟。  
"Brother,  
你不能这么想。  
you cannot think like that.  
天意谁也猜不透。  
No one can read Heaven's will.  
说不定最后。  
For all we know,  
偏偏就是小白登位。  
Xiaobai may be the one who takes the throne.  
你若不去。  
If you do not go,  
万一真是他当国君。  
and he really does become ruler,  
咱们这辈子。  
then all our lives  
就只能一直做破士了。  
we will remain nothing more than shabby *shi*.  
你必须去。  
You must go.  
你去了。  
If you go,  
我们才算双保险。  
only then do we have two layers of protection."  
他还答应。  
He also promised  
以后一定互相照应。  
that he would help whenever needed.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`宾语从句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `if you do not go ... we will remain nothing more than shabby shi`。<br>
难点拆解：前面先用 `For all we know` 引出“不好说”的语气，后面连续用了两个条件层次：`if you do not go` 和 `if he really does become ruler`；结果部分是 `we will remain...`，说明不去的后果。<br>
常用短语：
- `For all we know`：说不定、谁也说不好
- `take the throne`：登上王位

于是鲍叔牙。  
So Bao Shuya  
去做了公子小白的老师。  
became the tutor of Prince Xiaobai.  
管仲则找来好友召忽。  
Guan Zhong, meanwhile, invited his friend Zhao Hu  
一起辅佐公子纠。  
to help Prince Jiu together.  
两个公子。  
Both princes  
原本正愁没人帮。  
had been worrying that no one would stand by them.  
如今突然有人投来。  
So when capable men suddenly came to them,  
自然又感激又高兴。  
they were naturally grateful（感激的） and delighted.  

没过多久。  
Not long after,  
管仲又来找鲍叔牙。  
Guan Zhong sought Bao Shuya out again.  
他看出形势。  
He had read the situation  
开始不对劲了。  
and sensed that things were turning dangerous.  
他提醒鲍叔牙。  
He warned Bao Shuya,  
襄公最近多疑。  
"Duke Xiang has grown suspicious lately.  
这对公子纠。  
That is bad for Prince Jiu  
和公子小白都很不利。  
and equally bad for Prince Xiaobai.  
依我看。  
In my view,  
我们该先带他们。  
we should take them away for a while  
出去避一避。  
and hide outside the state.  
等齐国真的出事。  
When trouble truly breaks out in Qi,  
再找机会回来。  
we can return and seize the moment."  
其实他完全可以。  
In truth, he could have  
只提醒自己那一边。  
warned only his own side.  
可他转念一想。  
But then another thought came to him.  
如果小白被杀。  
If Xiaobai were killed,  
而公子纠又出意外。  
and Prince Jiu later died by some accident,  
那就全盘落空了。  
everything would collapse into nothing.  
所以两个都得保住。  
So both princes had to be kept alive.  
鲍叔牙当然。  
Bao Shuya, of course,  
还是那句好好。  
answered with the same ready "All right."  
对管仲的话。  
As far as Guan Zhong's advice went,  
他向来照单全收。  
he accepted it wholesale（全盘接受）.  
几天后。  
A few days later,  
公子纠和公子小白。  
Prince Jiu and Prince Xiaobai  
在同一天逃离齐国。  
fled Qi on the same day.  
公子纠的母亲。  
Prince Jiu's mother  
是鲁国人。  
was from Lu.  
将来还能借鲁国之力。  
That meant he might later borrow Lu's support.  
所以他跟着。  
So he went with  
管仲和召忽。  
Guan Zhong and Zhao Hu  
投奔了鲁国。  
to seek refuge（避难） in Lu.  
鲁庄公果然。  
Duke Zhuang of Lu  
热情接待了他们。  
received them warmly.  
公子小白的母亲。  
Prince Xiaobai's mother  
则是卫国人。  
was from Wei.  
可鲍叔牙没有选卫国。  
But Bao Shuya did not choose Wei.  
他带着小白。  
Instead, he took Xiaobai  
去了附近的莒国。  
to the nearby state of Ju.  
那只是个小国。  
It was only a small state.  
为什么这么选？  
Why choose it?  
鲍叔牙说得很清楚。  
Bao Shuya's reasoning was clear.  
第一。  
First,  
我们是避难。  
we are seeking shelter,  
不是畏罪潜逃。  
not fleeing as criminals.  
襄公不会追捕我们。  
Duke Xiang will not hunt us down,  
所以去莒国也安全。  
so Ju is safe enough.  
第二。  
Second,  
正因为莒国小。  
precisely because Ju is small,  
我们去了以后。  
once we arrive there,  
他们更不敢怠慢。  
they will be even less likely to slight（怠慢） us.  
第三。  
Third, and most importantly,  
莒国离临淄更近。  
Ju lies closer to Linzi.  
真要机会来了。  
If opportunity appears,  
我们可以更快赶回去。  
we can hurry back much faster.  
管仲和鲍叔牙。  
Guan Zhong and Bao Shuya  
在避难问题上。  
thus adopted different strategies（策略）  
采取了不同策略。  
for going into exile（流亡）.  
因为两位公子的处境。  
That was because the two princes  
本来就不一样。  
stood in different situations.  
事实后来证明。  
Later events proved  
他们的判断都没错。  
that both judgments were sound.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`原因状语从句`、`时间状语从句`、`条件状语从句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `precisely because Ju is small, once we arrive there, they will be even less likely to slight us`。<br>
难点拆解：`because Ju is small` 说明原因，`once we arrive there` 表示时间条件，真正的结果落在 `they will be ...`；这种层层推进的结构很适合表达“先解释原因，再落到结果”。<br>
常用短语：
- `be less likely to do`：更不容易做……
- `hurry back`：赶快返回

### 生死时速

后来齐襄公果然被杀。  
In time, Duke Xiang was indeed killed.  
可机会先被无知抢走。  
But the opportunity was seized first by Wuzhi,  
无知先做了国君。  
who claimed the throne himself.  
公子小白有点后悔。  
Prince Xiaobai began to feel some regret.  
他问鲍叔牙。  
He asked Bao Shuya,  
当初要是不跑。  
"If we had not fled back then,  
是不是就轮不到无知了？  
would Wuzhi have missed his chance?"  
鲍叔牙回答得很干脆。  
Bao Shuya answered crisply,  
当初若不走。  
"If we had not left,  
命都没了。  
you might not even be alive now.  
放心吧。  
Do not worry.  
他坐不了几天。  
He will not stay there long."  
其实鲍叔牙自己。  
In truth, Bao Shuya  
心里也想过这个问题。  
had asked himself the same question.  
可他还是相信。  
But he still trusted  
管仲不会看错。  
that Guan Zhong had not misread the situation.  

算辈分的话。  
By family reckoning,  
管至父还是管仲的堂叔。  
Guan Zhifu was actually Guan Zhong's cousin-uncle.  
他杀襄公。  
After helping kill Duke Xiang  
又立无知之后。  
and enthroning Wuzhi,  
做了齐国上卿。  
he became the top minister of Qi.  
这时他想起了管仲。  
At that point he remembered Guan Zhong.  
他知道管仲有才。  
He knew Guan Zhong had talent,  
便派人去鲁国。  
so he sent envoys to Lu  
请他回齐。  
to invite him back to Qi,  
还许他做上大夫。  
promising him a senior post.  
管仲一口回绝。  
Guan Zhong refused outright.  
回去告诉叔叔。  
"Go back and tell my uncle  
先把自己的脑袋看好。  
to keep a close watch on his own head."  
事实证明。  
Events soon proved  
这回他又说对了。  
that he was right once again.  

无知从登基。  
From enthronement（登基） to death,  
到最后被杀。  
from accession（即位） to assassination（刺杀）,  
前后只坐了。  
Wuzhi sat on the throne  
一个多月。  
for barely more than a month.  
无知被杀那天。  
On the very day Wuzhi was killed,  
两匹快马。  
two fast horses  
从临淄分头出发。  
raced out of Linzi in opposite directions,  
一匹去鲁国。  
one bound for Lu,  
一匹去莒国。  
the other for Ju.  
原来管仲和鲍叔牙。  
Guan Zhong and Bao Shuya  
都在临淄布了眼线。  
had both planted informants（眼线） in Linzi.  
就是为了第一时间。  
That was how they could learn  
拿到消息。  
the news at the very first moment.  
信息有多重要。  
If anyone doubts how important information is,  
看看这里就知道了。  
this episode answers the question.  
早在春秋。  
As early as the Spring and Autumn period,  
他们两人。  
both men  
就已经把情报。  
had already treated intelligence（情报）  
看得极重。  
as something of the highest importance.  
这一次。  
This time,  
两人几乎同时。  
they received the report almost simultaneously（同时地）.  
而且都明白。  
And both understood  
真正的机会。  
that the real opportunity  
终于来了。  
had finally arrived.  

接下来。  
What mattered next  
就看他们如何。  
was how each of them  
处理这条消息。  
handled that information.  
管仲立刻带着。  
Guan Zhong immediately took  
公子纠去鲁国后宫。  
Prince Jiu into the inner palace of Lu  
拜见文姜。  
to see Wen Jiang.  
齐襄公死后。  
After Duke Xiang's death,  
文姜已经回到曲阜。

公子纠见了她。  
When Prince Jiu saw her,  
也不绕弯子。  
he did not waste time.  
姐姐，你得帮我。  
"Sister, you must help me.

and claim the throne."  
文姜答应得很快。  
Wen Jiang agreed at once.  
好，我帮你。  
"All right, I will help."  
毕竟公子纠的母亲。  
After all, Prince Jiu's mother  
是鲁国人。  
was from Lu,  
她自然更有亲近感。  
so Wen Jiang felt a stronger kinship（亲近感） toward him.  
随后文姜。  
Wen Jiang then  
带着公子纠。  
took Prince Jiu  
去见鲁庄公。  
to see Duke Zhuang of Lu.  
鲁庄公一听。  
As soon as Duke Zhuang heard the request,  
当然愿意。  
he was naturally willing.  
公子纠既是亲戚。  
Prince Jiu was family,  
这个人情又大。  
and the favor would be substantial（大的）.  
若真扶他上位。  
If he truly helped him onto the throne,  
以后鲁齐关系。  
relations between Lu and Qi  
岂不是更牢？  
would become even tighter.  
可这时有人反对。  
But someone objected.  
这个人就是施伯。  
That man was Shi Bo.  
他劝鲁庄公。  
He advised Duke Zhuang,  
此事不可轻动。  
"Do not act rashly（轻举妄动地） in this matter.  
齐鲁表面上。  
Qi and Lu may appear  
世代联姻。  
to be linked by generations of marriage,  
实际上却是大敌。  
but in truth they are major rivals.  
如今齐国无主。  
Qi is leaderless now.  
这对鲁国。  
That is good news  
本来是好事。  
for Lu.  
不如先静观其变。

若齐国以后。  
If Qi later asks us  
求我们送回公子纠。  
to return Prince Jiu,  
那就是欠下。  
it will owe us  
天大的人情。  
an enormous debt of gratitude.  
若公子小白先回去。  
If Prince Xiaobai returns first  
做了国君。  
and becomes ruler,  
我们手里还有公子纠。  
we will still have Prince Jiu here.  
正好可以给他们。  
He can serve as  
不断添乱。  
a constant source of trouble for them."  
施伯向来。  
Shi Bo was known  
被称为鲁国智者。  
as a wise man of Lu,  
这主意也确实。  
and the idea was indeed  
又毒又稳。  
both cunning（狡猾的） and prudent（稳妥的）.  
可鲁庄公。  
But Duke Zhuang  
已经拿定主意。  
had already made up his mind.  
他亲自率领。  
He personally led  
三百乘战车。  
three hundred chariots  
护送公子纠。  
to escort Prince Jiu  
浩浩荡荡。  
in imposing（声势浩大的） formation  
向齐国进发。  
toward Qi.  

鲁庄公为什么。  
Why did he make such a grand display?  
一来鲁国是大国。  
First, Lu was a major state.  
做事不能偷偷摸摸。

二来当时齐国。  
Second, Qi had no ruler at the moment,  
又没有国君。  
so Lu's three hundred chariots  
三百乘车。  
would face no real rival.  
还有一点。  
There was also a third point.  
公子纠是老二。  
Prince Jiu was the elder of the surviving brothers,  
公子小白是老三。  
while Prince Xiaobai was the younger.  
按资历。  
By seniority（资历）,  
本来也该轮到公子纠。  
it should have been Prince Jiu's turn.  
这样大张旗鼓过去。  
Marching in with such fanfare（招摇）  
等于提前告诉齐人。  
was like telling the people of Qi in advance,  
你们的国君回来了。  
"Your ruler has returned."  
可管仲还不放心。  
But Guan Zhong still was not reassured.  
他向鲁庄公请命。  
He asked Duke Zhuang for a separate mission.  
公子小白在莒国。  
"Prince Xiaobai is in Ju.  
他一定会来争位。  
He will certainly contest the throne.  
而且他离临淄。  
And he is closer to Linzi  
比我们更近。  
than we are.  
请给我三十乘车。  
Give me thirty chariots,  
我去半路截住他。  
and I will intercept（拦截） him on the road."  
鲁庄公觉得。  
Duke Zhuang found the proposal reasonable（合理的）.  
于是先拨给他。  
So he first assigned him  
三十乘战车。  
thirty chariots  
让他立刻出发。  
and sent him off at once.  
其余大军。  
The rest of the army  
则由自己。  
would follow  
随后推进。  
under his own command.  

鲍叔牙这边。  
On Bao Shuya's side,  
接到消息后。  
once the news arrived,  
他马上带着公子小白。  
he immediately took Prince Xiaobai  
去见莒国国君。  
to meet the ruler of Ju.  
他们要回齐。  
They explained that they were returning to Qi  
争夺君位。  
to compete for the throne,  
希望借些兵马。  
and asked to borrow troops.  
莒君听了。  
The lord of Ju was delighted.  
他当然乐意。  
He was naturally happy to help.  
若是自己扶立了。  
If he helped establish  
齐国的新国君。  
the new ruler of Qi,  
以后就等于。  
he would be embracing  
抱上了一棵大树。  
a mighty patron（靠山）.  
鲍叔牙却很冷静。  
Bao Shuya, however, stayed cool-headed.  
他不要全国动员。  
He did not want the entire state mobilized（动员）.  
只要一百乘战车。  
He asked for only a hundred chariots.  
因为他很清楚。  
Because he knew perfectly well

what they needed to win was time,  
不是声势。  
not spectacle（排场）.  
莒国全部兵力。  
Even Ju's full military strength  
也不到两百乘。

真和鲁国硬碰。

根本没有胜算。  
they had no real chance.  
他们唯一的优势。

就是先机。  
was being first.  
于是莒君。  
So the lord of Ju  
立刻准备好。  
quickly prepared  
一百乘战车。  
a hundred chariots.  
鲍叔牙不敢耽误。
