当即带着小白。  
He set out at once with Xiaobai  
踏上归途。  
and started the return journey.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`主语从句`、`条件状语从句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `what they needed to win was time, not spectacle`。<br>
难点拆解：这里的 `what they needed to win` 是 `what` 引导的主语从句，整体作主语；后面用 `was time, not spectacle` 做表语说明真正关键的是什么。再往下的 `If they collided...` 是条件状语从句，补充说明为什么时间比声势更重要。<br>
常用短语：
- `need to win`：赢下来所需要的
- `head-on`：正面地、硬碰硬地

### 管仲被骗

鲍叔牙和公子小白。  
Bao Shuya and Prince Xiaobai  
果然抢在了前面。  
did indeed get ahead.  
他们一路急赶。  
They pressed forward at full speed  
到了临朐。  
until they reached Linqu.  
这时人马都累坏了。  
By then both men and horses were exhausted.  
只得停下埋锅做饭。  
So they stopped to cook a meal  
稍稍歇一口气。  
and catch a brief breath.  

管仲这边。  
Guan Zhong, on the other hand,  
算得很精。  
had calculated everything carefully.  
他知道鲍叔牙。  
He knew Bao Shuya  
一定会立刻动身。  
would set out immediately.  
也知道莒国军队。  
He also knew that the army of Ju,  
无论战马、战车。  
in horses, chariots,  
还是士兵素质。  
and the quality of its soldiers,  
都比不上鲁军。  
fell far short of Lu's forces.  
他甚至暗中。  
He had even secretly  
做过路线和速度的实验。  
run practical tests on routes and speed.  
通过实地测算。  
Based on those field calculations,  
他认定自己。  
he concluded  
完全可以在临朐。  
that he could definitely catch them  
追上鲍叔牙和小白。  
at Linqu.  
所以他没有。  
That is why he did not  
直奔莒国。  
head straight for Ju.  
而是直接插向。  
Instead, he cut directly  
临朐这条线。  
toward Linqu.  
该算到的。  
Everything that could be calculated,  
他几乎都算到了。  
he had almost calculated.  
偏偏就差一步。  
And yet one crucial step  
他还是没算到。  
still escaped him.  

等管仲率军。  
When Guan Zhong finally led the Lu force  
来到临朐。  
to Linqu,  
正好遇见莒军。  
he found the Ju troops  
正在埋锅造饭。  
cooking their meal.  
鲍叔牙和小白。  
Bao Shuya and Xiaobai  
则坐在车上。  
were seated in their chariot,  
焦急地等着。  
waiting anxiously.  
管仲命车停下。  
Guan Zhong ordered his chariot to halt.  
自己单车上前。  
Then he rode forward alone.  
那边一看是他。  
The moment the other side recognized him,  
小白和鲍叔牙。  
both Xiaobai and Bao Shuya  
都吃了一惊。  
were startled.  
管仲高声问。  
Guan Zhong called out loudly,  
公子这么着急。  
"Prince, why such haste?  
这是要去哪儿？  
Where are you rushing off to?"  
小白回答。  
Xiaobai answered,  
回去奔丧。  
"Back to attend a funeral."  
至于是给谁奔丧。  
As for whose funeral,  
他自己都未必清楚。  
he probably did not know himself.  
管仲顺口接道。  
Guan Zhong replied at once,  
既然是奔丧。  
"If it is a funeral,  
那也该公子纠先去。  
then Prince Jiu, as the elder, should go first.  
你先等等吧。  
You had better wait."  
至于是谁的丧。  
As for whose mourning it was,  
他其实也不知道。  
he did not know that either.  
只是张口就来。  
He was simply making it up on the spot.  

小白一下子。  
Xiaobai did not know  
不知道该怎么接话。  
how to answer.  
只好去看鲍叔牙。  
So he looked to Bao Shuya.  
鲍叔牙很干脆。  
Bao Shuya cut straight to the point.  
如今各为其主。  
"We now serve different masters.  
废话少说。  
Enough talk.  
你赶紧走。  
Leave at once,  
再不走我就动手。  
or I will make a move."  
他知道和管仲。  
He knew that in a contest of wits  
比心眼未必能赢。  
he might not beat Guan Zhong,  
不如直接来硬的。  
so blunt force was safer.  
管仲看了看形势。  
Guan Zhong sized up the situation.  
自己只有三十乘车。  
He had only thirty chariots.  
对方差不多一百乘。  
The other side had close to a hundred.  
真要打起来。  
If a fight truly broke out,  
自己肯定吃亏。  
he would certainly be at a disadvantage.  
到那时再第一个逃。  
And if he then had to flee first yet again,  
场面未免太难看。  
it would look uncomfortably embarrassing（难堪的）.  
所谓好汉不吃眼前亏。  
A wise man does not pick a losing fight in front of him.  
就算鲍叔牙。  
Even though Bao Shuya  
一向把他当偶像。  
had always admired him,  
真到了各为其主的时候。  
once they stood on opposite sides,  
对方也绝不会客气。  
the man would show no mercy.  
管仲只得。  
So Guan Zhong  
拨转车头后撤。  
turned his chariot around and withdrew.  
可没走多远。  
But after going only a short distance,  
他忽然。  
he suddenly  
取弓搭箭。  
took up his bow, nocked（搭上） an arrow,  
转身就向小白射去。  
and wheeled around to shoot at Xiaobai.  
管仲的箭术。  
Guan Zhong's archery  
一向很准。  
was famously precise（精准的）.  
这一箭。  
This arrow  
不偏不倚。  
flew dead on target  
正中小白胸前。  
and struck Xiaobai in the chest.  
小白惨叫一声。  
Xiaobai cried out,  
口吐鲜血。  
spat blood,  
随后倒在车上。  
and collapsed in the chariot.  
管仲见状大笑。  
Seeing this, Guan Zhong burst into laughter.  
他一边笑。  
He laughed as he drove away,  
一边急速撤走。  
rapidly retreating.  
身后三十乘鲁车。  
Behind him,  
也跟着一起离开。  
the thirty Lu chariots withdrew as well.  
他心里得意。  
He was delighted with himself.  
这下你还争什么。  
"Let's see how you contest the throne now.  
公子纠可以。  
Prince Jiu can now  
稳稳当当地。  
ride steadily  
去临淄做国君了。  
to Linzi and become ruler."  
可这一回。  
Yet this time,  
管仲终于算错了。  
Guan Zhong had finally miscalculated.  
也许在他一生里。  
Perhaps in his whole life,  
这就是他唯一一次。  
this was the only time  
真正输给鲍叔牙。  
he truly lost to Bao Shuya.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`时间状语从句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `if a fight truly broke out, he would certainly be at a disadvantage`。<br>
难点拆解：`if a fight truly broke out` 是虚拟意味较强的条件结构，表示“真打起来的话”；后面又接了一个并列条件 `if he then had to flee...`，继续说明更尴尬的后果。`once they stood on opposite sides` 则是时间状语从句，交代双方关系已经变化。<br>
常用短语：
- `be at a disadvantage`：处于劣势
- `show no mercy`：毫不留情

## 第二十六章 鲍叔牙的妙计
## Chapter 26: Bao Shuya's Brilliant Plan

眼看小白中箭。  
Seeing Xiaobai struck by the arrow,  
鲍叔牙都呆住了。  
Bao Shuya froze in shock.  
他怎么也没想到。  
He had never imagined  
这一次。  
that this time  
自己还是输给了管仲。  
he would still lose to Guan Zhong.  
他想哭都哭不出来。  
He was too stunned even to cry.  
只能恨恨地看着。  
He could only watch bitterly  
管仲的人马。  
as Guan Zhong and his men  
越跑越远。  
rode farther and farther away  
直到消失不见。  
until they vanished.  

“走远了吗？”  
"Have they gone far enough?"  
忽然。  
All at once,  
车里传来小白的声音。  
Xiaobai's voice came from the chariot.  
“什么？”  
"What?"  
鲍叔牙大吃一惊。  
Bao Shuya stared in disbelief（不敢相信）.  
小白竟然没死。  
Xiaobai was not dead.  
“走远了吗？”  
"Have they gone far enough?"  
这一次。  
This time,  
他的声音更清楚了。  
his voice was even clearer.  
而且十分镇定。  
It was calm and steady,  
一点也不像受了伤。  
nothing like the voice of a wounded man.  
“远了。”  
"Far enough,"  
鲍叔牙这才回过神。  
Bao Shuya finally answered.  
小白慢慢坐起身。  
Xiaobai slowly sat up.  
手里还捏着那支箭。  
He was still holding the arrow in his hand.  
“真险。  
"That was close.  
幸亏带钩挡住了。”  
Luckily the belt hook stopped it."  
原来那支箭。  
As it turned out,  
正打在他衣服上的带钩上。  
the arrow had struck the hook on his belt,  
并没有真的伤到他。  
and had not truly wounded him.  
那一瞬间。  
In that split second,  
小白急中生智。  
Xiaobai improvised（急中生智） at once.  
立刻装成中箭。  
He pretended to have been hit,  
倒在车上。  
collapsed inside the chariot,  
也顺便躲过了。  
and at the same time  
管仲可能补来的第二箭。  
avoided any second shot Guan Zhong might have fired.  
“那口血呢？”  
"And what about the blood?"  
鲍叔牙忍不住问。  
Bao Shuya could not help asking.  
“我把舌头咬破了。”  
"I bit my tongue."  
鲍叔牙又是一惊。  
Bao Shuya was startled again.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`表语从句`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `if the ruler of Qi is installed by Lu, they will certainly name an outrageous price and bleed Qi badly`。<br>
难点拆解：前面 `if the ruler of Qi is installed by Lu` 是条件状语从句，说明“如果由鲁国扶立”；后面主句有两个并列动作 `name` 和 `bleed`，一起说明后果。前一句 `Prince Xiaobai is still the better choice` 则是表判断的核心句。<br>
常用短语：
- `be installed by`：被……扶立
- `name a price`：开价、提条件

常用短语：
- `remain alive`：继续活着
- `reel from`：因……而发懵、受重创

作者先借一首歌开场。

常用短语：
- `make use of`：利用、重用

Though the prison cart  
一点也不舒服。  
was anything but comfortable,  
管仲的心情。  
Guan Zhong's spirits  
还是轻松了一些。  
did lighten somewhat.  
出发前。  
Even before setting out,  
他从看守士兵。  
he had already gathered from the guards' gossip  
闲聊的话里。  
that he was still in real danger.  
听出了不对。  
He knew  
施伯是个聪明人。  
Shi Bo was far too clever  
绝不会轻易。  
to let him  
让自己活着回齐国。  
go back to Qi alive without trying something.  
只要他脚下。  
So long as his feet  
还没踏上齐国土地。  
had not yet touched Qi soil,  
危险就始终存在。  
the danger remained.  
押送他的士兵。  
The soldiers escorting him  
却一点也不着急。  
seemed to feel no urgency at all.  
别人急。  
Everyone else might be in a hurry,  
他们不急。  
but they were not.  
管仲很清楚。  
Guan Zhong understood perfectly  
必须想办法。  
that he had to find a way  
让这些人快起来。  
to make them move faster.  
对逃命专家管跑跑来说。  
For Guan the Runner, that expert in survival,  
只要事关逃命。  
whenever life and escape were involved,  
总会有办法。  
there was always a method.  

他先开口说。  
He began by saying,  
弟兄们。  
"Brothers,  
你们听过。  
have you ever heard  
那个齐国姑娘的故事吗？  
the story of that girl from Qi?"  
一提姑娘。  
The moment a girl was mentioned,  
男人们果然都来了兴趣。  
the men, as expected, were interested.  
于是管仲。  
So Guan Zhong  
开始一路讲段子。  
started telling jokes all along the road.  
其中当然。  
Naturally,  
还夹着不少黄段子。  
quite a few of them were rather bawdy（下流的）.  
很快。  
Soon,  
路上有了笑声。  
laughter filled the road.  
大家赶路。  
The march  
也没那么闷了。  
felt far less dull.  
人一轻松。  
Once the men relaxed,  
车也跑快了。  
the carts began to move faster too.  
可再好笑的段子。  
But even the best jokes  
也有讲完的时候。  
eventually run out.  
何况一路上。  
Besides,  
总是他一个人说。  
he had been the only one talking.  
嘴也干了。  
His mouth was drying out,  
嗓子也快哑了。  
and his throat was nearly gone.  
怎么办？  
What then?  
逃命专家。  
The escape expert  
还有第二招。  
had a second trick.  
这一招就是。  
He would get  
让大家的嘴。  
everyone else's mouth moving too.  
也都动起来。  
That meant making them sing.  
照今天的话说。  
In today's terms,  
管仲既是作词人。  
Guan Zhong was both a lyricist（作词人）  
也是作曲人。  
and a composer（作曲家）.  
真要放到现在。  
If he lived in our age,  
他在写歌这件事上。  
he might have held  
大概也得有。  
roughly the stature（地位）  
李宗盛那个级别。  
of a Li Zongsheng.  

管仲当场。  
Right there on the road,  
写出一首《黄鹄》。  
Guan Zhong improvised a song called *Yellow Swan*.  
歌词很古。  
Its wording was ancient.  
这里就不逐句抄了。  
I will not copy it line by line here.  
大意是。  
Its meaning, roughly, was this.  
他把自己比作。  
He compared himself  
一只困在笼中的黄鹄。  
to a yellow swan trapped in a cage,  
有翅难飞。  
with wings yet no freedom to fly,  
有本事也施展不开。  
and gifts with no chance to use them.  
他也不知道。  
He did not know  
一旦破笼而出。  
whether, once the cage broke open,  
自己会一飞冲天。  
he would soar straight into the sky  
还是已经。  
or whether  
连斗志都磨没了。  
his spirit had already been worn away.  
这样的歌。  
A song like that  
若是你怀才不遇。  
would suit anyone  
或者正受挫折。  
who felt unseen or crushed by setbacks（挫折）.  
唱一唱还真合适。  
It would fit the mood perfectly.  
当然。  
Of course,  
拿来安慰股市失手。  
it is probably less suited  
就未必合适了。  
to consoling yourself after a bad run in the stock market.  
这首歌的曲调。  
The melody itself  
慷慨又悲凉。  
was stirring（激昂的） yet bleak（苍凉的）.  
一会儿高。  
At times it rose high,  
一会儿低。  
at times it sank low.  
而且特别顺口。  
And it was wonderfully catchy（上口的）.  
没过多久。  
Before long,  
士兵们全学会了。  
the soldiers had all learned it.  
大家一路唱着。  
They sang as they went,  
赶车也更起劲了。  
and drove the carts with new energy.  
本来一天的路。  
What should have taken a full day  
半天就走完了。  
was finished in half.  
天黑时。  
By nightfall,  
管仲已经进了。  
Guan Zhong had already entered  
齐国地界。  
the territory of Qi.  
他这才。  
Only then  
深深松了一口气。  
did he let out a deep breath.  
而就在这时。  
And just then,  
三乘鲁国战车。  
three Lu chariots  
才赶到边界。  
finally reached the border.  
他们奉命来干什么？  
What had they been sent to do?  
追杀管仲。  
To hunt Guan Zhong down.  
可惜还是慢了一步。  
But they were one step too late.  
隰朋和鲁庄公。  
Xipeng's long conversation  
那场有意拖延的闲聊。  
with Duke Zhuang, which had been meant to delay him,  
成功拖住了。  
had successfully held back  
施伯继续劝说鲁庄公。  
Shi Bo's next push to persuade the duke.  
虽然最后。  
Though Duke Zhuang eventually  
鲁庄公还是接受了。  
did accept Shi Bo's fierce advice,  
施伯的强烈建议。  
by then  
可一切都晚了。  
everything was already too late.  
换个角度看。  
Seen from another angle,  
一个新时代既然来了。  
once a new age had truly arrived,  
鲁庄公本来也拦不住。  
Duke Zhuang was never going to stop it.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`时间状语从句`、`条件状语从句`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `so long as his feet had not yet touched Qi soil, the danger remained`。<br>
难点拆解：`so long as` 引出条件，表示“只要还没踏上齐国土地”；后面的 `the danger remained` 是结果主干。前文 `he had already gathered from the guards' gossip that...` 又用宾语从句交代了他为什么如此警觉。<br>
常用短语：
- `for the time being`：暂时
- `let out a deep breath`：长出一口气

### 朋友

鲍叔牙早就在青州等着。  
Bao Shuya had long been waiting in Qingzhou.  
等得久了。  
He had waited so long  
连心都焦了。  
that his heart had begun to burn with worry.  
终于。  
At last,  
鲁国的囚车到了。  
the prison cart from Lu arrived.  
车上带来。  
It brought  
两颗脑袋。  
two severed heads  
和一个活人。  
and one living man.  
鲍叔牙根本不关心。  
Bao Shuya had no interest at all  
那两颗脑袋。  
in whether the two heads  
是真是假。  
were genuine（真的） or not.  
他只在乎。  
He cared only  
那个活人。  
whether the living man  
是不是管仲。  
was truly Guan Zhong.  
一看见他。  
The moment he saw him,  
鲍叔牙就感叹。  
Bao Shuya sighed,  
谢天谢地。  
"Thank Heaven, thank Earth,  
也谢人啊。  
and thank the people as well."  
囚车立刻被打开。  
The prison cart was opened at once.  
锁镣也立刻解开。  
The shackles（镣铐） were taken off just as quickly.  
老仲。  
"Old Zhong,  
受苦了。  
you have suffered."  
鲍叔牙一边说。  
As he spoke,  
一边亲手把管仲。  
Bao Shuya personally helped Guan Zhong  
从车里扶出来。  
down from the cart.  
叔牙。  
"Shuya,  
我实在惭愧。  
I am ashamed,"  
管仲低声说。  
Guan Zhong said quietly.  
这份惭愧。  
And the shame  
还真不是装的。  
was no act.  
鲍叔牙却误会了。  
Bao Shuya misunderstood him.  
惭愧什么？  
"Ashamed of what?  
这不都在。  
Wasn't all of this  
你当初计划之中吗？  
within your original plan?  
咱们俩分头守着。  
The two of us guarded different sides.  
谁赢。  
Whoever won,  
不都是一样？”  
wasn't it all the same?"  
他以为管仲。  
He thought Guan Zhong  
只是因为。  
was merely ashamed  
输给了自己。  
of losing to him.  
管仲摇了摇头。  
Guan Zhong shook his head.  
不是这个。  
"Not that.  
当初咱们分头守着。  
When we divided ourselves between the two princes,  
这样的结局。  
we had already foreseen  
其实早就料到了。  
that an ending like this might come.  
我惭愧的。  
What shames me  
不是结果。  
is not the result.  
是召忽尽忠而死。  
It is that Zhao Hu died in loyalty,  
我却苟且偷生。  
while I lived on in compromise（苟且）.  
只怕他地下有知。  
If he knows it in the underworld,  
也要笑我。”  
he will surely laugh at me."  
他不是为输赢惭愧。  
He was not ashamed of winning or losing.  
他是为自己。  
He was ashamed  
活下来的方式惭愧。  
of the way he had stayed alive.  
鲍叔牙继续劝他。  
Bao Shuya kept counseling（开导） him.  
成大事的人。  
"A man meant for great things  
不该计较小耻。  
cannot fuss over small shames.  
立大功的人。  
A man meant for great achievements（功业）  
也不能被。  
cannot let himself  
一点小节绊住。  
be trapped by minor scruples（顾忌）.  
老仲。  
Old Zhong,  
你的才华。  
your talent  
本就该拿来。  
was made  
替国家出力。  
for serving a state.  
怎么能为了。  
How could you  
一个人去死呢？”  
throw it away for a single man?"  
其实这些道理。  
In truth,  
管仲哪里会不懂。  
Guan Zhong understood all that perfectly well.  
他懂得。  
He understood it  
只会比鲍叔牙更多。  
even better than Bao Shuya did.  
不然的话。  
Otherwise,  
他又怎么会。  
how could he have  
总能在关键时刻。  
managed, at every critical moment,  
先把命保下来？  
to keep himself alive?  
问题在于。  
The issue was this.  
道理归道理。  
Knowing the truth is one thing.  
轮到自己头上。  
When it comes to yourself,  
还是得有人。  
you still need someone else  
替你把话说出来。  
to say it aloud for you.  
那样你才觉得。  
Only then do you feel  
有人懂你。  
that someone understands you,  
面子也留住了。  
that your face is saved,  
心里也有台阶下。  
and that your heart has found a step to come down from.  

第二天。  
The next day,  
鲍叔牙下令。  
Bao Shuya ordered  
大军撤离鲁国边境。  
the army withdrawn from the border of Lu.  
自己则带着管仲。  
He himself brought Guan Zhong  
先回临淄。  
back toward Linzi,  
并安排他。  
first placing him  
住进自己。  
in his own villa（别墅）  
郊外的别墅。  
outside the city.  
随后。  
Then  
鲍叔牙独自。  
Bao Shuya went alone  
去见齐桓公。  
to see Duke Huan.  
一见面。  
The moment he arrived,  
他还故意卖了个关子。  
he deliberately played a little game of suspense（悬念）.  
主公。  
"My lord,  
坏消息和好消息。  
I have bad news and good news.  
您先听哪个？  
Which would you like first?"  
坏的。  
"The bad."  
齐桓公很紧张。  
Duke Huan sounded genuinely nervous.  
鲍叔牙说。  
Bao Shuya replied,  
坏消息是。  
"The bad news is  
你哥哥被人杀了。”  
that your elder brother has been killed."  
齐桓公一愣。  
Duke Huan paused.  
哦。  
"Oh.  
这消息。  
That is  
还真挺坏。  
indeed bad news."  
可他心里。  
But in his heart,  
明显松了一口气。  
he had obviously relaxed.  
这算什么坏消息？  
What kind of bad news was that, really?  
那好消息呢？  
"Then what is the good news?"  
我把管仲。  
"I have brought Guan Zhong  
给你带回来了。  
back to you."  
太好了。  
"Excellent.  
这可真是好消息。  
That really is good news.  
我要亲手宰了他。”  
I will kill him with my own hands."  
齐桓公恨恨地说。  
Duke Huan said it with genuine bitterness（恨意）.  
这对他来说。  
For him,  
还真算得上好消息。  
it truly did count as good news.  
鲍叔牙却立刻拦住。  
But Bao Shuya stopped him at once.  
不行。  
"No.  
你不能杀他。”  
You must not kill him."  
为什么？  
"Why not?  
难道就因为。  
Is it simply  
你们交情太深？”  
because the two of you are such close friends?"  
不是。  
"No.  
是因为。  
It is because  
你能有今天。  
you owe where you stand today  
本来就该谢他。”  
to him."  
为什么？  
"Why?"  
鲍叔牙便一条条。  
Bao Shuya then laid it out item by item.  
当初让我。  
"It was his idea  
来辅佐你。  
that I should support you.  
让我们一起。  
It was his idea  
逃到国外避难。  
that we should flee abroad for safety.  
知道无知被杀后。  
When Wuzhi was killed,  
我们要立刻赶回临淄。  
our rush back to Linzi  
那个判断。  
grew from methods  
也是我跟他。  
I had learned  
做生意时学来的。  
from doing business with him.  
就连说服。  
Even the way  
国高两家的手法。  
I persuaded the Guo and Gao houses  
也是他教我的。  
came from him.  
说到底。  
To put it plainly,  
没有管仲。  
without Guan Zhong,  
就没有今天的我们。”  
there would be no us today."  
齐桓公还是不甘心。  
Duke Huan still was not satisfied.  
那他射我那一箭。  
"And what about the arrow he shot at me?  
就这么算了？”  
Do we simply forget that?"  
鲍叔牙只好。  
Bao Shuya could only press the point harder.  
主公。  
"My lord,  
管仲的才能。  
Guan Zhong's talent  
是我远远比不上的。  
is far beyond anything I can match.  
如果主公只是想。  
If your lordship wants only  
把齐国治理好。  
to govern Qi well,  
让百姓安居乐业。  
to let the people live in peace and prosperity（繁荣）,  
我们不惹别人。  
to avoid provoking others  
别人也不来惹我们。  
and to avoid being provoked in return,  
那眼前这些人。  
then the men you already have  
也就够用了。  
are enough.  
可是如果主公。  
But if your lordship  
还想称霸天下。  
means to dominate the realm,  
那就非管仲不可。”  
then only Guan Zhong will do."  
齐桓公半信半疑。  
Duke Huan was only half convinced.  
真有这么厉害？  
"Is he really that formidable（厉害的）?  
他还能比师傅你更强？”  
Can he truly be stronger than you, Teacher?"  
不过想了想。  
Still, after thinking it over,  
他还是退了一步。  
he gave way a little.  
那就先赦他不死吧。  
"Very well. Then let us spare his life for now."  
鲍叔牙已经很高兴了。  
Bao Shuya was already pleased with that.  
能先保住。  
If he could first preserve  
管仲的命。  
Guan Zhong's life,  
对他来说。  
that alone  
就已经值了。  
was worth a great deal.  
他很清楚。  
He understood perfectly  
事情都得一步步来。  
that all things had to be done step by step.  
急不得。  
There was no rushing them.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`并列句`、`表语从句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `if your lordship wants only to govern Qi well ... the men you already have are enough, but if your lordship means to dominate the realm, then only Guan Zhong will do`。<br>
难点拆解：这句话用了两个并列的 `if` 条件结构，前半句讲“只想治好齐国”的情况，后半句讲“想称霸天下”的情况；两个结果分别是 `are enough` 和 `only Guan Zhong will do`，对比非常鲜明。<br>
常用短语：
- `for now`：暂时、眼下
- `give way`：让步

三天之后。  
Three days later,  
齐桓公开始。  
Duke Huan began  
论功行赏。  
to reward merit.  
有人发财。  
Some got rich.  
有人升官。  
Some were promoted.  
国家和高家。  
The Guo house and the Gao house  
功劳最大。  
had earned the greatest merit.  
各自都分到了。  
Each received  
不少好田好地。  
fine fields and valuable land.  
其他功臣。  
The other meritorious men  
也都各有封赏。  
were all rewarded as well.  
齐桓公接着说。  
Then Duke Huan said,  
老师。  
"Teacher,  
上卿这个位子。  
the office of chief minister  
就是您的了。”  
shall be yours."  
他想让鲍叔牙。  
He wanted Bao Shuya  
出任总理。  
to serve as prime minister.  
鲍叔牙却一口回绝。  
Bao Shuya refused outright.  
不要。  
"No.  
说实话。  
To be honest,  
你给我一口饭吃。  
if you give me a bowl of rice,  
我就已经感激了。  
I am already grateful.  
不是我不想做上卿。  
It is not that I do not want the office.  
是我没那个本事。  
It is that I do not have the ability for it.  
我的本事。  
My own talent  
也就是守规矩。  
goes no further than obeying rules  
做个安分人。”  
and being a decent man."  
齐桓公不信。  
Duke Huan did not believe him.  
老师太谦虚了。  
"Teacher, you are too modest."  
鲍叔牙却说。  
Bao Shuya answered,  
我不是谦虚。  
"This is not modesty.  
是真的不行。  
I truly am not equal to it.  
能做上卿的人。  
The man who can truly serve as chief minister  
得让国内百姓。  
must steady the people at home,  
让四方邻国。  
calm the neighboring states,  
让王室受益。  
bring benefit to the royal house,  
让诸侯受泽。  
spread advantage among the feudal lords,  
让国家稳如泰山。  
make the state as secure as Mount Tai,  
让君主享长久之福。  
and secure lasting good fortune for his ruler.  
他的功劳。  
His merit  
要能刻进金石。  
must deserve to be carved into bronze and stone,  
他的名声。  
and his name  
要能传到千古。  
must travel through the ages.  
这样的位子。  
A post like that  
我拿不起。”  
is not one I can carry."  
作者顺手解释。  
The author helpfully glosses the point.  
说白了。  
To put it plainly,  
总理这个人。  
the right prime minister  
得能让上头高兴。  
must satisfy those above,  
让下头服气。  
earn the trust of those below,  
让邻居敬畏。  
command respect from the neighbors,  
也让外敌发怵。  
and make foreign enemies nervous.  
要拿今天的话说。  
In modern language,  
没有这个人。  
without such a man,  
你顶多只是个大户。  
you may at best be a wealthy player.  
有了这个人。  
With him,  
你才有资格。  
you gain the right  
去做那个坐庄的人。  
to run the table yourself.  
齐桓公听到这里。  
Hearing this,  
马上追问。  
Duke Huan asked at once,  
那你说。  
"Then tell me,  
谁行？”  
who can do it?"  
鲍叔牙只说了两个字。  
Bao Shuya answered in two words:  
管仲。  
"Guan Zhong."  
齐桓公终于被打动。  
Duke Huan was finally moved.  
好。  
"All right.  
既然老师。  
Since Teacher  
一再说他行。  
has repeatedly said he is the one,  
那就把他叫来。  
bring him in.  
我亲自跟他谈。  
I will speak with him myself  
看看他到底如何。”  
and see what he is truly made of."

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`并列句`、`宾语从句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `if those above observe proper standards, kinship ties remain firm, and if ritual, righteousness, integrity, and shame are all upheld, the ruler's commands will naturally prevail`。<br>
难点拆解：这段话本身就是并列结构很多的治国总纲，前后用了两个 `if` 条件句；第一个落到 `kinship ties remain firm`，第二个落到 `the ruler's commands will naturally prevail`。前面多组短句如 `When the granaries are full...` 也是同样的条件推结果结构，读起来要抓住“条件+结果”的节奏。<br>
常用短语：
- `pay close attention to`：密切注意
- `come down to`：归结为

## 第二十八章 管子思想
## Chapter 28: The Thought of Master Guan

毫无疑问。  
There is no doubt  
管仲是春秋时代。  
that Guan Zhong was  
最伟大的政治家。  
the greatest statesman（政治家） of the Spring and Autumn age,  
而且不是“之一”。  
and not merely one of the greatest.  
他彻底改造了齐国。  
He completely transformed（改造） Qi,  
也彻底改写了。  
and he also rewrote  
春秋的历史走向。  
the course of Spring and Autumn history.  
从那以后。  
After him,  
整个春秋战国的政治家。

几乎都受过他的影响。

说得公允一点。  
To speak fairly,  
管仲是在发扬。  
Guan Zhong was extending

and putting into practice the thought of the Duke of Zhou.  
他把那些理念。  
He took those ideas  
建设性地用在。

诸侯国的治理上。  
to the governance（治理） of a feudal state.  
更重要的是。  
More importantly,  
他对人民的重视。  
his concern for the people,  
以及对法治的重视。  
and his concern for rule by law,  
都比前人更进了一层。  
both rose to a new level.  
他的管理模式里。  
In his system of administration（管理）,  
有大量数据。  
he used large amounts of data,  
也有很多“工艺”。  
and a great deal of technical method.  
他不只在政治上创新。

在农业、商业、军事。  
but also in agriculture（农业）, commerce（商业）, and military affairs,

如果真有时间。  
If you have the time,  
最好找来《管子》。

你就会发现。

管子的很多思想。

### 四项基本原则

管子先立了四维。  
Master Guan first established four pillars（支柱）.  
礼、义、廉、耻。  
They were ritual, righteousness, integrity（廉）, and shame.  
他认为。  
He believed  
少一维。  
that if one pillar was lost,  
国家就会倾斜。  
the state would tilt.  
少两维。  
If two were lost,  
国家就危险。  
the state would fall into danger.  
少三维。  
If three were lost,  
国家就会颠覆。  
the state would be overturned.  
四维都没了。  
If all four vanished,  
国家就会灭亡。  
the state itself would perish（灭亡）.  
他又进一步解释。  
He then explained further.  
有礼。  
With ritual,  
人就不会越过。  
people would not overstep  
应有的分寸。  
the proper limits.  
有义。  
With righteousness,  
人就不会妄自钻进。  
people would not push themselves forward improperly.  
有廉。  
With integrity,  
人就不会遮掩恶行。  
people would not cover up wrongdoing.  
有耻。  
With shame,  
人就不会跟着歪路走。  
people would not follow crooked ways.  
所以有了四维。  
So once the four pillars were in place,  
国家就有秩序。  
the state gained order,  
邪乱的事。  
and corrupt（邪恶的） disorder  
也就不容易发生。  
found less room to arise.  
这四样。  
These four things  
就是管子的基本原则。  
were Master Guan's basic principles.  
国家想发展。  
If a state wished to develop,  
人民想富裕。  
and if its people wished to prosper,  
这四条。  
these four principles  
就是起步的根基。  
formed the necessary foundation.  
礼义廉耻。  
Ritual, righteousness, integrity, and shame  
后来也成了。  
would later become  
儒家思想的重要地基。  
part of the very ground of Confucian thought.  

### 以民为天

齐桓公问管仲。  
Duke Huan once asked Guan Zhong,  
做国君最该尊重什么？  
"What should a ruler respect above all?"  
管仲回答。  
Guan Zhong answered,  
天。  
"Heaven."  
齐桓公抬头。  
Duke Huan looked up  
真的去看天。  
to the sky quite literally.  
管仲笑了。  
Guan Zhong laughed.  
我说的不是头顶的天。  
"I do not mean the sky above your head.  
我说的是百姓。  
I mean the people.  
君主要以民为天。  
A ruler must treat the people as Heaven.  
百姓爱戴你。  
If the people cherish（爱戴） you,  
国家就安定。  
the state will be stable.  
百姓帮助你。  
If the people help you,  
国家就强大。  
the state will be strong.  
百姓不满你。  
If the people resent you,  
国家就危险。  
the state will be in danger.  
百姓厌弃你。  
If the people cast you aside,  
国家就会灭亡。”  
the state will perish."  
“以民为天”这句话。  
That phrase, "take the people as Heaven,"  
直到今天。  
even today  
听起来仍然了不起。  
still sounds extraordinary（非凡的）.  
后来很多民选领袖。  
Many later elected leaders  
说自己以选民为天。  
have said they take their voters as Heaven.  
其实根子。  
In spirit,  
都能追到管仲这里。  
the root of that idea can be traced back to Guan Zhong.  

那么怎么做。  
So how is that principle  
才算真的以民为天？  
actually put into practice?  
管仲给出的答案是。  
Guan Zhong's answer was simple.  
政策能推行。  
What makes policy work  
靠的是顺民心。  
is that it follows the people's hearts.  
政策会废掉。  
What destroys policy  
往往是逆民心。  
is that it goes against them.  
也就是说。  
In other words,  
做政策之前。  
before making policy,  
要先站在百姓那边想。  
one must first think from the people's side.  
要让百姓。  
Policy should aim  
过得安稳。  
to leave them safe,  
过得舒心。  
at ease,  
过得富足。  
and well provided for.  
政策落地之前。  
Before it is implemented,  
还要广泛听意见。  
their views should also be widely heard.  
凡是对百姓有利的。  
Whatever truly benefits the people  
就坚决去做。  
must be done firmly.  
凡是对百姓不利的。  
Whatever harms the people  
就坚决别做。  
must be firmly avoided.  
管仲还说。  
Guan Zhong also said  
治国先富民。  
that governing a state begins with enriching the people.  
先让百姓富起来。  
Let the people become prosperous first.  
然后国家。  
Only then  
才谈得上真正强大。  
can the state become genuinely strong.  
这一点。  
On this point,  
他和姜太公。  
he and Jiang Taigong  
其实很一致。  
were very much of one mind.  
国富民穷。  
A rich state with poor people  
既危险。  
is dangerous,  
也丢脸。  
and it is shameful too.  
要先藏富于民。  
Wealth must first be stored among the people.  
有了这个前提。  
With that as the premise（前提）,  
治理才有基础。  
good governance has a base,  
国家也才能长久。  
and the state can endure.  
若国家老和百姓争利。  
If the state constantly competes with the people for profit,  
哪怕看起来强。  
then even if it looks powerful for a time,  
也只是短命的强。  
that strength will not last.  
所以从周公。  
So from the Duke of Zhou  
到管仲。  
to Guan Zhong,  
民本思想。  
people-centered thought  
确实又往前迈了一步。  
truly took another step forward.  
不仅更充实。  
It became not only fuller,  
也更具体。  
but more concrete（具体的）.  
不过要说明。  
Still, one distinction matters.  
管子的“敬民为天”。  
Guan Zhong's "revering the people as Heaven"  
和后世那种。  
is not the same  
“爱民如子”。  
as the later slogan of "loving the people like children."  
不是一回事。  
They are not the same idea at all.  
跟管仲比。  
Compared with Guan Zhong,  
后世很多说法。  
many later formulas  
反而像在往后退。  
look almost like a retreat（倒退）.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `if the people cherish you, the state will be stable; if the people help you, the state will be strong`。<br>
难点拆解：这里是一组平行结构，连续用多个 `if` 条件句来推导国家状态；读的时候抓住“百姓的态度”决定“国家的命运”这个对应关系，就不容易乱。<br>
常用短语：
- `stand on someone's side`：站在某人一边
- `for a time`：一时、暂时

### 以德治国

先说“德”。  
Let us first clarify the word "virtue."  
这里说的德。  
Here, virtue  
不是个人品德。  
does not mean personal morality（品德）.  
它更接近。  
It is closer  
德政这个意思。  
to benevolent government.  
若德只指品德。  
If virtue meant only private character,  
那齐桓公这种。  
then a ruler like Duke Huan,  
好酒又好色的人。  
who loved wine and women,  
根本没法治理国家。  
could never have governed a state.  
所以关键不在。  
So the key lies not in  
君主嘴上说什么。  
what a ruler says,  
而在他真做了什么。  
but in what he actually does.  
德政这个概念。  
The idea of benevolent government  
是接着“以民为天”来的。  
grows directly out of taking the people as Heaven.  
今天常说。  
Today we often speak of  
为百姓办实事。  
doing real things for ordinary people.  
这也算德政。  
That too belongs to benevolent government,  
不过只是一小部分。  
but only as a small part of it.  
和管仲所说的。  
Compared with what Guan Zhong meant,  
根本不是一个量级。  
it is not even on the same scale（层级）.  

管仲把德政。  
Guan Zhong divided benevolent government  
具体分成六兴。  
into six forms of flourishing.  
第一兴。  
The first  
是让百姓。  
was to improve the people's livelihood（生计）.  
生活厚起来。  
That meant opening fields,  
开田。  
building homes,  
建屋。  
studying cultivation,  
讲究种植。  
encouraging farming,  
鼓励耕作。  
and repairing houses.  
第二兴。  
The second  
是给百姓。  
was to send wealth to the people.  
把财路做出来。  
That meant opening sources of wealth,  
广开财源。  
sorting out storage,  
整顿库藏。  
修路。  
building roads,  
便利贸易。  
and making trade convenient.  
第三兴。  
The third  
是把便利。  
was to leave practical benefit behind for the people.  
留给百姓。  
That meant draining stagnant water,  
疏水。  
opening channels,  
通河。  
修沟。  
repairing ditches,  
造桥。  
and building bridges.  
第四兴。  
The fourth  
是政治宽一点。  
was to make politics more lenient（宽松的）.  
少收税。  
Collect lighter taxes,  
少征赋。  
impose fewer levies,  
刑法简一点。  
simplify punishments,  
轻罪能赦就赦。  
and pardon lighter offenses where possible.  
第五兴。  
The fifth  
是救急。  
was to rescue those in urgent need.  
尊养老人。  
Honor the aged,  
收养孤儿。  
care for orphans,  
救济鳏寡。  
relieve widows and widowers,  
照顾病人。  
look after the sick,  
吊问灾祸。  
and console those struck by misfortune.  
第六兴。  
The sixth  
是扶贫。  
was to aid the poor.  
冷了给衣。  
Give clothes to the cold,  
饿了给饭。  
food to the hungry,  
穷了给救济。  
and relief to the destitute（赤贫者）.  
有了德政。  
Once benevolent government is in place,  
还要再督促人们。  
people must also be guided  
去守“七义”。  
to uphold the "seven duties."  
目的是什么？  
And why?  
是为了。  
To build  
一个和谐的社会。  
a harmonious（和谐的） society.  
再往下。  
After that,  
还要整顿“八经”。  
the "eight norms" must also be set in order.  
上和下。  
Above and below,  
都守礼。  
all should keep ritual order.  
贵和贱。  
Noble and humble,  
各安本分。  
each should remain within proper role.  
长和幼。  
Old and young  
都守次序。  
should keep their proper sequence.  
贫和富。  
Poor and rich alike  
都守法律。  
should live under the law.  
可以说。  
It can fairly be said  
在管仲的治国方案里。  
that throughout Guan Zhong's whole strategy of governance,  
德政是一直穿着的那条线。  
benevolent government is the thread running through everything.  
所以即便他自己。  
That is why, even though he himself  
生活有些奢侈。  
lived rather luxuriously（奢侈地）,  
也没有多少人。  
few people  
真去指责他。  
seriously condemned（指责） him.  
因为他让大家。  
Because he made life  
都过得更好了。  
better for everyone else.  

### 以法治国

说到法。  
When it came to law,  
管仲讲得更多。  
Guan Zhong had even more to say.  
内容也更完整。  
And what he said was remarkably complete.  
从法律为何重要。  
He covered why law mattered,  
到制度怎么建立。  
how institutions（制度） should be built,  
再到法律怎么执行。  
and how law should actually be enforced（执行）.  
几乎都讲到了。  
He touched nearly every part of it.  
随便摘几条。  
Take only a few examples,  
都能看出来。  
and you can already see  
他是个货真价实的法家。  
that he was a genuine Legalist（法家人物）.  
后来的法家。  
Later Legalists  
很多理论。  
would build many of their theories  
其实就是从这里长出来的。  
from this very foundation.  

管仲强调。  
Guan Zhong stressed  
法律要正。  
that law must be upright,  
制度要明。  
that institutions must be clear,  
有罪该杀。  
that the guilty must be punished,  
执行一定要真。  
and that enforcement must be real.  
只要刑罚。  
If punishment  
公正又说到做到。  
is just and actually carried out,  
百姓自然会敬畏。  
the people will naturally stand in awe.  
权威一旦立住。  
Once authority is established,  
命令就不用。  
orders need not be  
一遍遍重申。  
repeated again and again.  
换成今天的话。  
In today's language,  
这就是严格执法。  
that means strict enforcement,  
也是公开执法。  
and public enforcement as well.  
他还说。  
He also argued  
国君如果能统一立法。  
that if the ruler legislates（立法） in a unified way,  
百官就更容易守法。  
officials will be far more able to follow the law.  
上面若把制度。  
If those above openly lay out the system,  
明明白白摆出来。  
plainly and visibly,  
下面做事。  
those below  
才更容易。  
can act  
合于规则。  
within the rules.  
反过来。  
By contrast,  
如果上面自己。  
if those above themselves  
立法都不统一。  
cannot keep legislation consistent（统一的）,  
下面的人。  
then those below  
就会绕开公法。  
will start bypassing public law  
去搞私下那一套。  
and inventing private standards of their own.  
这说白了。  
Put plainly,  
就是要公开。  
this means law should be public,  
公平。  
fair,  
公正。  
and just.  
也就是让权力。  
It means power  
尽量放在阳光下运行。  
should operate in the sunlight as much as possible.  

管仲还把法令。  
Guan Zhong also placed commands and laws  
放到极高的位置。  
in an extremely high position.  
在他看来。  
In his view,  
治理百姓的根本。  
the root of governing the people  
没有比法令更要紧的。  
had nothing more important than law and command.  
随意改法。  
To alter the law casually,  
擅自加法。  
to add to it at will,  
扣着法令不发。  
to hold commands back,  
或者根本不执行。  
or simply refuse to enforce them,  
都不行。  
none of these could be allowed.  
法律有了权威。  
Once law has authority,  
下面的人。  
those below  
才会真正畏惧。  
will truly fear it.  
当然。  
Of course,  
严刑峻法。  
harsh punishment and severe law  
放在今天。  
raise questions of fit  
会有适用性的问题。  
in the modern world.  
但放回当时。  
But in that age,  
它自有时代意义。  
they had their own historical meaning.  

更有意思的是。  
What is even more interesting  
管仲对法律细节。  
is how subtle Guan Zhong could be  
也想得很细。  
about legal details.  
比如法令。  
For instance,  
若还没有公布。  
if a law has not yet been announced,  
有人提前做了。  
and someone happens to do what that future law requires,  
国君也不该。  
the ruler should not  
因此去奖赏他。  
reward him for that.  
否则守法的人。  
Otherwise, those who obey the rules  
就会觉得委屈。  
will feel wronged.  
而聪明人。  
And clever opportunists（投机者）  
就会开始猜。  
will begin guessing  
下一个法令会是什么。  
what the next decree may be,  
再提前表演。  
and performing in advance for favor.  
那样国家。  
That, in turn,  
反而更容易乱。  
makes disorder more likely.  
同样。  
Likewise,  
若法令还没公布。  
if a law has not yet been made public,  
有人碰巧触犯了。  
and someone happens to violate it,  
也不能立刻罚。  
he must not be punished at once.  
不然百姓。  
Otherwise the people  
就不知道自己。  
will no longer know  
什么能做。  
what they are allowed to do,  
什么不能做。  
and what they are not.  
安全感也就没了。  
Their sense of security（安全感） will vanish,  
社会反而更不稳。  
and society will grow less stable instead.  
这样的法治理解。  
This understanding of rule by law  
真的很深。  
is astonishingly deep.  
就算把他。  
Even if you placed him  
放到今天。  
in today's world,  
去法学院讲课。  
and asked him to lecture at a law school,  
也未必会显得落后。  
he might not look outdated at all.  
正是从管子开始。  
It was really from Master Guan onward  
齐国才建立起。  
that Qi established  
一套更严格的刑法。  
a more rigorous（严格的） criminal law.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `if a law has not yet been announced ... the ruler should not reward him`。<br>
难点拆解：这部分法律思想很像案例推理，先给出条件 `if a law has not yet been announced`，再给出结论；后面又用 `Otherwise...` 推出反面后果。读这种句子时，先抓条件，再抓后果，会清楚很多。<br>
常用短语：
- `at will`：任意地、随意地
- `in turn`：反过来、进而

### 哲学思想

管子的哲学。  
Master Guan's philosophy（哲学）  
也非常深。  
was also profound（深刻的）.  
《管子》一书里。  
Within the *Guanzi*,  
至少有九章。  
at least nine chapters  
专门讲哲学问题。  
are devoted specifically to philosophical issues.  
除此之外。  
And beyond those,  
其他很多篇里。  
many other sections  
也都顺带谈到。  
touch on philosophy as well.  
其中最重要的。  
One of the most important concepts  
就是“道”。  
is the Dao.  
这个概念。  
This concept  
管子讲得很早。  
was discussed by Master Guan very early,  
也讲得很系统。  
and with real system.  
后来老子写《道德经》。  
When Laozi later wrote the *Dao De Jing*,  
其中关于“道”的许多展开。  
many of its later developments on the Dao  
其实都能看见。  
still show  
承接管子的影子。  
the trace of Guan Zhong's treatment.  
管仲多次说。  
Guan Zhong says in many places  
道离人不远。  
that the Dao is not far from human life,  
却不容易真正够到。  
yet it is hard to reach in full.  
道无形无状。  
It has no form and no shape,  
可安于其中。  
one may dwell in it,  
却很难把它。  
yet it is hard  
完全说清。  
to state it completely.  
道就在天地之间。  
The Dao stands within heaven and earth.  
它大到无外。  
It is so great that nothing lies outside it,  
小到无内。  
and so small that nothing lies within beyond it.  
当然。  
Of course,  
关于“道”的解释。  
there are far more explanations of the Dao  
远不止这些。  
than these few lines.  
这里不一一列。  
There is no need to list them one by one here.  
大体上说。  
Broadly speaking,  
管子的哲学。  
Master Guan's philosophy  
带有很强的辩证色彩。  
has a strongly dialectical（辩证的） character,  
也含有一部分。  
and it also contains  
朴素的唯物意味。  
a strain of simple materialism（唯物主义）.  
后来《道德经》。  
Later, the *Dao De Jing*  
更多发扬了。  
developed more of  
他辩证的一面。  
that dialectical side,  
而《墨子》。  
while the *Mozi*  
则更偏向。  
leans more heavily  
他偏务实的那一面。  
toward his practical and material side.  
除了大讲“道”。  
Besides speaking at length about the Dao,  
管子还给。  
Master Guan also helped define  
很多哲学词。  
many philosophical terms.  
像规律。  
Concepts like pattern,  
规范。  
norm,  
势。  
and strategic force  
这些词的意思。  
all owe part of their later meaning  
都能追到他这里。  
to early definition here.  
若说管子。  
If one calls Master Guan  
是伟大的哲学家。  
a great philosopher,  
那真不算夸大。  
that is hardly an exaggeration（夸张）.  

### 军事思想

管仲也是军事奇才。  
Guan Zhong was also a military prodigy（奇才）.  
他讲兵。  
When he spoke of war,  
非常成体系。  
he did so with remarkable structure.  
在他看来。  
In his view,  
真正成就霸业。  
the shape of hegemony（霸业）  
先要靠德义。  
must first rest on virtue and justice,  
再靠智谋。  
then on strategy,  
再靠兵战。  
then on arms,  
再看地形。  
then on terrain,  
最后还看。  
and finally on  
具体行动是否得法。  
whether actual operations are properly handled.  
他并不喜欢。  
He was not a man  
动不动就打仗。  
who loved to rush into war.  
他的排序是。  
His order of preference was clear.  
先以德服人。  
First win people through virtue,  
其次用谋。  
then use strategy,  
再次外交。  
then diplomacy（外交）,  
最后才出兵。  
and only last send troops.  
也因此。  
For that reason,  
管仲不是总劝。  
Guan Zhong was not always urging  
齐桓公去征讨谁。  
Duke Huan to campaign against others.  
恰恰相反。  
Quite the reverse,  
他常常劝。  
he often urged  
齐桓公少出兵。  
him not to go to war.  
不论对大国小国。  
Whether dealing with large states or small ones,  
他都先试。  
he first tried  
和平办法。  
peaceful means.  
另一条基本原则。  
Another basic principle  
是不打没准备的仗。  
was never to fight an unprepared war,  
也不打没把握的仗。  
and never to fight a war one had no confidence in winning.  
在他的指挥下。  
Under his direction,  
齐军没有打过败仗。  
Qi's armies did not taste defeat.  
而且他对。  
And on matters like  
赏罚。  
rewards and punishments,  
军费。  
military finance,  
编制。  
organization,  
号令。  
signals and commands,  
武器。  
weapons,  
地图。  
maps,  
敌情。  
and intelligence on the enemy,  
几乎每一项。  
he had concrete requirements  
都有很细的要求。  
for almost every detail.  
“知己知彼”。  
"Know yourself and know your enemy"  
这种观念。  
as a military principle  
在他这里。  
already has clear roots  
就已经很清楚了。  
in his thought.  
可以说《孙子兵法》。  
One may fairly say that *The Art of War*  
就是在很大程度上。  
in large part  
整理并继承了。  
organizes and inherits  
管仲的军事思想。  
Guan Zhong's military thought.  

### 市场经济

先富民。  
Enrich the people first.  
后强国。  
Strengthen the state after.  
顺着这个思路。  
Following that logic,  
管仲的经济策略。  
Guan Zhong's economic strategy  
很明确。  
was very clear.  
农业要重视。  
Agriculture had to be valued,  
商业也要鼓励。  
and commerce had to be encouraged as well.  
既然要鼓励种地。  
If people were to be encouraged to farm,  
农业税就该减。  
then agricultural taxes should be reduced.  
问题是。  
The question, then,  
农业税减了以后。  
was what would support  
国家财政靠什么？  
the state treasury once those taxes fell.  
管仲的答案是。  
Guan Zhong's answer  
从商业里找。  
was to look to commerce.  
尤其是盐和铁。  
Salt and iron in particular  
成了财政的两大支柱。  
became the two main pillars of state finance.  
但除了盐铁。  
But beyond salt and iron,  
他在更大范围里。  
he adopted, in broader terms,  
实行的是。  
something very close to  
一种很彻底的市场经济。  
a robust market economy（市场经济）.  
商业税。  
Commercial taxes  
征得很少。  
were kept very low.  
市场运行。  
Market operations  
干预得也很少。  
were interfered with only lightly.  
让市场自己。  
Let the market itself  
决定买卖的命运。  
decide the fate of trade,  
让竞争去决定。  
and let competition decide  
谁是强者。  
who proves stronger.  
不仅如此。  
Not only that,  
他还懂得。  
he also knew  
如何利用市场。  
how to use the market  
去控制甚至干预。  
to control or even influence  
敌国的政府。  
the governments of rival states.  
对外贸易。  
Foreign trade, too,  
也是他很看重的一块。  
was something he took very seriously.  
齐国的通关费用。  
Qi kept customs charges low,  
又便宜。  
cheap,  
手续又简。  
and procedures simple.  
结果很快就成了。  
The result was that it quickly became  
整个周朝的。  
the economic and trade center  
经贸中心。  
of the whole Zhou world.  
集市在他眼里。  
In his eyes, the market fair  
是市场经济的重要标志。

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`宾语从句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `if he formed a cabinet simply to share the meat with his friends, how could he possibly help you govern the state well`。<br>
难点拆解：前半句 `if he formed...` 是条件状语从句，后半句是反问式主句，用来强调“真正能治国的人不能任人唯亲”；后面又用多个 `that... was Heaven's will` 这种宾语从句来支撑鲍叔牙的态度。<br>
常用短语：
- `take offense`：生气、介意
- `contend with`：与……争、与……抗衡

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`宾语从句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `if after that they still insist on invading, then justice will be on our side`。<br>
难点拆解：这句话里 `if after that they still insist on invading` 是条件状语从句，主句是 `justice will be on our side`；后面再接 `our chance of victory will be far greater`，属于并列结果。<br>
常用短语：
- `step back gracefully`：体面退下
- `on the spot`：当场、现场

### 曹刿论战

that is, 684 BC,  
也是齐桓公二年。  
the second year of Duke Huan of Qi.

their second great clash,

the second  
山东德比。  
Shandong derby（山东德比）.

自己曾在乾时。

慢着。

Duke Zhuang gave the order at once.  
鲁军鼓声齐起。  
Lu's drums answered as one,  
大军随即冲锋。  
and the army charged forward.  
士气正盛。  
Its morale（士气） was at full tide.  
齐军也开始冲。  
Qi's army also began to advance,  
可明显看得出来。  
but one could plainly see  
它已经乱了。  
that it had fallen into disorder.  
有人还在等。  
Some soldiers were still waiting  
第四通鼓。  
for a fourth drumbeat.  
有人已经冲出去了。  
Others had already rushed ahead.  
有人冲得快。  
Some ran fast.  
有人冲得慢。  
Some lagged behind.  
两军还没真正撞上。  
Before the two sides had even collided,  
胜负其实。  
the outcome  
已经差不多定了。  
was almost settled.  
等真的一交手。  
Once they actually met,  
齐军立刻。  
Qi's army  
就溃成了一团。  
collapsed into full rout（溃败）.  
士兵争先逃命。  
Its soldiers fled for their lives.  
鲁庄公一看。  
The duke, seeing this,  
大手一挥。  
swung his arm  
就想追击。  
and prepared to pursue.  
曹刿急忙拦住。  
Cao Gui hurriedly stopped him.  
等等。  
"Wait.  
我先看看。”  
Let me look first."  
他下了车。  
He stepped down from the chariot,  
装模作样。  
put on a show of inspection,  
看了看战场。  
and surveyed the field.  
然后说。  
Then he said,  
可以追了。  
"Now we may pursue."  
于是鲁军。  
So Lu's troops  
一路猛追。  
chased hard,  
一口气把齐军。  
and drove the Qi army  
赶回了齐国。  
all the way back toward Qi.  

打赢之后。  
After the victory,  
鲁庄公当然要请教。  
Duke Zhuang naturally asked  
曹刿到底是怎么想的。  
what exactly Cao Gui had seen.  
于是就有了。  
And thus there arose  
那段著名的。  
that famous discussion  
“曹刿论战”。  
known as "Cao Gui on War."  
曹刿的核心意思。  
The heart of Cao Gui's answer  
其实很简单。  
was actually quite simple.  
打仗。  
War  
拼的就是一口气。  
is a contest of courage and momentum（气势）.  
第一通鼓。  
At the first drumbeat,  
士气最足。  
spirit is fullest.  
第二次。  
By the second,  
就已经衰了。  
it has begun to decline.  
到了第三次。  
By the third,  
那股气。  
that force  
也就快竭了。  
is close to spent.  
等他们三鼓而衰。  
So when their strength had ebbed after three drumbeats,  
我们这边。  
while ours  
正好一鼓作气。  
was rising fresh at the first,  
赢面自然就在我们。  
victory naturally leaned  
这一边了。  
toward our side.  
至于后来。  
As for the pursuit afterward,  
曹刿又说。  
Cao Gui gave another reason.  
大国不好猜。  
A great state is hard to read.  
谁知道它。  
Who could say  
会不会还有伏兵？  
whether it still had an ambush in reserve?  
所以他才下车。  
That is why he climbed down  
先去看。  
and checked first.  
等他看见。  
When he saw  
车辙已乱。  
that their wheel tracks were in chaos  
大旗也都倒了。  
and their banners had sagged,  
他就明白。  
he understood  
那不是诱敌。  
that this was no feigned retreat,  
而是真败。  
but a real collapse.  
这才放心追。  
Only then did he allow the pursuit.  
鲁庄公听完。  
After hearing this,  
大喜过望。  
Duke Zhuang was overjoyed.  
曹先生。  
"Master Cao,  
您真是太有才了。”  
you are astonishingly gifted."  
他当场任命曹刿。  
He at once appointed Cao Gui  
做了大夫。  
to the rank of *dafu*.  
从此曹刿。  
From then on,  
也正式进了。  
Cao Gui formally joined  
吃肉的阶层。  
the class that ate meat.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`时间状语从句`、`原因状语从句`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `when their strength had ebbed after three drumbeats, while ours was rising fresh at the first, victory naturally leaned toward our side`。<br>
难点拆解：这里用 `when` 表示时机，用 `while` 做对比，前半句讲敌军士气衰竭，后半句讲我方士气正旺，最终结果是 `victory ... leaned toward our side`。这也是曹刿判断战机的核心。<br>
常用短语：
- `at once`：立刻
- `in reserve`：留作后手、备用

### 齐宋联军

鲍叔牙大败而归。  
Bao Shuya returned in defeat.  
事实证明。  
Events had proved  
管仲是对的。  
that Guan Zhong had been right.  
可齐桓公。  
But Duke Huan  
心里很恼火。  
was furious.  
他又召集了。  
He summoned  
一次紧急内阁会议。  
another emergency cabinet meeting,  
而且是扩大的。  
and this one was enlarged,  
因为鲍叔牙。  
for Bao Shuya  
也被叫来了。  
was called in too.  
齐桓公一开口。  
Duke Huan began by saying,  
这是我登基以来。  
"This was my first real campaign  
第一次正式出兵。  
since taking the throne.  
结果却大败。  
Yet it ended in a great defeat.  
以后我还怎么。  
How am I supposed  
在诸侯面前。  
to present myself  
自称大国？”  
as a great state before the feudal lords now?"  
他的意思。  
Everyone understood  
大家都听得懂。  
what he was driving at.  
他还想打。  
He wanted to fight again.  
管仲还是反对。  
Guan Zhong opposed it once more.  
我认为。  
"I believe  
近期不宜出兵。  
that in the near term we should not go to war.  
我们应该。  
We should  
专心发展经济。  
focus on developing the economy.  
等国力强了。  
When the state grows stronger,  
鲁国自然会来臣服。”  
Lu will come and submit of its own accord."  
鲍叔牙却很没面子。  
But Bao Shuya had lost too much face.  
谁不想找回面子？  
Who does not want to recover his dignity?  
于是他提出。  
So he proposed another path.  
主公。  
"My lord,  
齐鲁两国。  
Qi and Lu  
都是千乘大国。  
are both great chariot states.  
实力本来就差不多。  
Their strength is roughly similar.  
谁占主场。  
Whoever has the home field  
谁就更占便宜。  
has the better chance.  
单靠我们自己。  
If we rely on ourselves alone,  
未必稳稳赢得了鲁国。  
we may not defeat Lu with certainty.  
不如联络宋国。  
Better to join with Song.  
两家联军。  
A joint force from two states  
就能压住鲁国。”  
can overwhelm Lu."  
他现在一门心思。  
By now  
就是还想打。  
he wanted only to fight again.  
可人一旦钻了牛角尖。  
Once a man gets trapped in an obsession（执念）,  
往往就危险了。  
things often turn dangerous.  
齐桓公立刻同意。  
Duke Huan agreed immediately.  
好主意。  
"A good idea.  
老师。  
Teacher,  
这件事。  
this matter  
就全权交给您。”  
I leave entirely in your hands."  
管仲听了。  
Guan Zhong heard this  
却只能沉默。  
and could only remain silent.  
他心里已经暗暗下了决心。  
Inwardly, he made a quiet decision.  
若再来一次。  
If something like this happened again,  
他就不干了。  
he would quit.  

宋国那边。  
Song replied quickly.  
很快就回了信。  
Its answer came back at once.  
他们很愿意。  
They were quite willing  
和齐国一起伐鲁。  
to campaign against Lu together with Qi.  
理由其实也简单。  
The reasons were simple enough.  
第一。  
First,  
宋齐关系。  
relations between Song and Qi  
一直还不错。  
had long been fairly good.  
第二。  
Second,  
宋国平时。  
Song had often  
没少受鲁国欺负。  
been pushed around by Lu.  
如今正好。  
Now it could  
借齐国的力气。  
borrow Qi's strength  
出一口气。  
and finally vent its anger.

The two states fixed a date.  
各出三百乘战车。

一同进攻鲁国。

宋国派出的主帅。

齐国这边。  
On Qi's side,  
仍然是鲍叔牙挂帅。

南宫长万。  
Nangong Changwan  
是宋国有名的大力士。  
was Song's famous strongman.  
力气大得惊人。

大致可以算。  
Roughly speaking,  
春秋版的施瓦辛格。  
he was a Spring and Autumn Schwarzenegger.  
手下先锋。

叫猛获。

光听名字。  
The name alone  
就已经够猛。  
sounded fierce enough.  

鲍叔牙春天。  
Bao Shuya had been beaten by Cao Gui

in the spring.  
到了夏天。  
By summer,  
他又带兵来了。  
he had come again with an army.  
串亲戚都没这么勤。

齐宋两军。  
The Qi and Song forces  
在郎城会合。

宋军驻在东南。

齐军驻在西北。

鲁庄公还是亲自出征。

因为上次打赢了齐军。  
Because he had beaten Qi last time,  
这次他的信心。  
his confidence  
更足了。  
was even stronger.  
鲁国五百乘战车。  
Lu's five hundred chariots  
浩浩荡荡赶到郎城。  
rolled grandly to Langcheng.  
大营背城而扎。

鲁庄公进城坐镇。  
Duke Zhuang entered the city to direct things,  
依旧带着曹刿。  
again with Cao Gui at his side.  
而鲁国大将公子偃。  
Yet Lu's general Prince Yan  
一直都不太服曹刿。  
had never really accepted Cao Gui.  
他心里想。  
In his mind he thought,  
我们这些公族。  
we princes of the ruling house  
从小练武。  
had trained in arms since childhood  
也读兵书。  
and studied military texts as well.  
难道真比不过。  
Could we truly be worse  
这个斗鸡的乡巴佬？  
than some village cockfighter?  
战前会议上。  
At the war council,  
鲁庄公先发问。  
Duke Zhuang put the question first.  
鲍叔牙。  
"Bao Shuya  
憋着一口气。  
is burning for revenge,  
宋国主帅南宫长万。  
and Song's commander Nangong Changwan  
又力大无穷。  
is immensely strong.  
现在两军。  
Now the two armies  
在城外互成犄角。  
have formed a horned position outside the city.  
我们怎么应对？  
How should we answer them?"  
曹刿没急着说。  
Cao Gui did not hurry to answer.  
他想先看形势。  
He preferred to read the situation first.  
倒是公子偃。  
Prince Yan, however,  
早准备好了。  
had come well prepared.  
主公。  
"My lord,  
我刚才出去看过。  
I have just gone out and looked.  
齐军戒备森严。  
Qi's camp is tightly guarded.  
队伍也很整齐。  
Its lines are orderly.  
显然是上次。  
It is obvious  
被打怕了。  
they were frightened by last time.  
可宋军不一样。  
But Song's troops are different.  
他们松松垮垮。  
They are slack and disorderly.  
南宫长万。  
Nangong Changwan  
仗着自己勇猛。  
trusts too much in his own strength.  
在那里来回炫耀。  
He struts back and forth showing off.  
只要主公。  
If your lordship gives me  
给我三百乘战车。  
three hundred chariots,  
我就悄悄。  
I will slip out quietly  
从雩门出去。  
through the Yu Gate,  
偷袭宋营。  
strike the Song camp,  
必定一举击溃他们。  
and crush them in one blow.  
宋军一垮。  
Once Song collapses,  
齐军自然也会退。”  
Qi will naturally retreat."  
公子偃这番功课。

可鲁庄公还是摇头。

不行。  
"No.  
你不是南宫长万的对手。”

可鲁庄公没想到。

是公子偃。  
was that Prince Yan  
准备得太足。  
had prepared too thoroughly  
也就胆子太大。  
and had therefore grown too bold.  
他竟然擅自调动。

and acted on his own.  
每辆战车前面的马。

he draped a tiger skin.  
一共一百张。

随后这一百乘车。  
Then the hundred chariots  
悄悄出了雩门。  
slipped quietly out through the Yu Gate  
像饿虎扑食一样。  
and rushed on the Song camp  
扑向宋营。  
like hungry tigers pouncing on prey.  
宋军做梦也没想到。  
Song's soldiers had never imagined  
会有人来劫营。

更糟的是。  
Worse still,  
他们的战马。  
their horses,  
一见鲁军马身上的虎皮。  
the moment they saw the tiger skins on Lu's horses,  
全都吓坏了。  
were terrified（惊恐的）.  
四处乱窜。

公子偃的鲁军。  
Prince Yan's men  
趁势横冲直撞。

宋营一下就乱成一团。  
The Song camp instantly fell apart.  
宋军只能。  
The Song troops  
纷纷逃命。  
could only flee for their lives.  

很快就有人。  
Word quickly reached  
把消息报到鲁庄公那里。  
Duke Zhuang.  
说公子偃。  
Prince Yan  
已经偷袭得手。  
had struck successfully.  
鲁庄公先是吃惊。  
The duke was first stunned,  
接着竟有点不知所措。  
then a little at a loss.  
曹刿立刻喊道。  
Cao Gui immediately shouted,  
主公。  
"My lord,  
机不可失。  
the chance must not be missed.  
立刻派大军。  
Send the main force at once  
去接应公子偃。  
to support Prince Yan.  
宋军一定会大败。”  
The Song army will surely collapse."

Duke Zhuang decided on the spot.  
亲自带两百乘战车。

to strike the Song camp.  
还留下两百乘。

随时准备拦齐军。  
to block Qi if needed.  
宋军本来就撑不住了。  
Song was already barely holding together.  
这时再被。  
Now, with two hundred more Lu chariots  
两百乘鲁军一压。  
crashing in,  
要是不崩。  
if it had not broken then,  
那才怪了。  
that would have been the real surprise.  
南宫长万。  
Nangong Changwan, however,  
仗着自己力大。

一点也不怕。

替宋军断后。

可鲁军一向。  
But Lu's soldiers had always  
就瞧不起宋军。

见他们溃逃。

哪里肯放过。  
they had no intention of letting them go.  
于是一路穷追。  
So they pursued without mercy  
一直追进了宋国。  
all the way into Song territory,  
追到乘丘。  
as far as Chengqiu.  

这一下。  
At that point,  
南宫长万彻底受不了了。

自己本来。  
He had marched out magnificently  
是浩浩荡荡。  
to punish Lu.  
去伐鲁的。

back onto his own soil.

这脸往哪放？

更何况。

他还号称。  
of greatest warrior under Heaven

他什么都顾不上了。  
he forgot everything else.  
直接掉转车头。

要承认。

鲁庄公越看越奇怪。

鲁庄公顺手。

在当时。

特点是。

was precision,

and range.

鲁庄公抄弓。

搭上金翎箭。

瞄准。

先进武器。

又稳又直。

and buried itself  
南宫长万的肩膀。

鲁军一拥而上。

就算是施瓦辛格。

has his bad days.  

宋国这个国家。

本来就很爱打仗。

真正打赢。

什么漂亮仗。

它的军工产业。

看上去简直像是。  
one might joke,  
专门给别国。

in a certain later era.  
更有趣的是。

等到宋国有一天。  
when Song finally did  
难得雄起一次。

它却又。

### 首先，构建和谐社会

国家怎么才能和谐？

怎么才能稳定？

管仲的答案是。  
Guan Zhong's answer was this:  
先把社会。  
first organize society  
分业管理。  
by occupation.  
他把百姓。  
He divided the people  
分成四类。  
into four groups:  
士、农、工、商。  
the *shi*, farmers, artisans, and merchants.

因为这四类人。  
Because these four groups  
职业不同。

different income,

最容易起冲突。

In the end,  
搞乱社会。  
that could throw society into disorder（混乱）.

再配上。

三国五鄙。

让士农工商。

各住各的。

各守各业。

父子相袭。  
and inherit those occupations from father to son.  
这样才好。

安居乐业。

四民之中。  
Among the four groups,  
士、工、商。  
the *shi*, artisans, and merchants

和十五个士乡。

总共二十一个乡。

five of them.

城内的编制。  
Inside the capital,

四里一连。  
four neighborhoods to a block,  
十连一乡。  
and ten blocks to a district.  
这就叫三其国。  
That was what "three in the capital" meant.  
每一级。  
Each level  
都设官长。  
had its own head,  
各负其责。  
and each bore its own responsibilities.  
每年正月。  
Every first month of the year,

汇报治理情况。  
their state of governance to the ruler.  

乡野里的农民。  
As for the farmers in the countryside,  
三十家为一邑。  
thirty households formed a hamlet,  
十邑为一卒。  
ten hamlets formed a unit,  
十卒为一乡。

各设五大夫。

每年正月。

这套办法。  
This system

这里的分居。

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`原因状语从句`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `Because these four groups had different work, different income, and different interests, conflict was easy`。<br>
难点拆解：这里用 `Because...` 先交代原因，后面接并列成分 `different work, different income, and different interests`，最后落到结果 `conflict was easy`；这种结构很适合写“先分析原因，再给出结论”的句子。<br>
常用短语：
- `keep to`：守住、坚持
- `sum up as`：概括为

土壤的成色。

还有很多。

and the importance of agriculture.  
在他看来。

历代能统一天下的君主。

国家富。  
their states were rich

而粮食。  
And grain

要么是地没有。

### 第三，减免农业税

正因为农业。  
Precisely because agriculture  
如此重要。  
was so important,  
农民的利益。  
the interests of farmers  
和他们的积极性。  
and their willingness to work  
也就变得极关键。  
became crucial（关键的） as well.  
管仲认为。  
Guan Zhong believed  
农业生产。  
that agriculture  
本来就受天时左右。  
is governed by the seasons and the weather.  
每年的收成。  
The harvest of each year  
基本是有上限的。  
has its natural limits.  
可一旦打仗。  
Yet once war breaks out,  
对粮食的需求。  
demand for grain  
会突然猛增。  
surges all at once.  
这时若再压农民。  
If the farmers are pressed at such a time,  
他们根本吃不消。  
they simply cannot bear it.  
所以国家。  
That is why the state  
一方面要有储备。  
must keep reserves on the one hand,  
另一方面也要少打仗。  
and wage fewer wars on the other.  
他还认为。  
He also believed  
国家财政。  
that state revenue  
不应该主要靠农业。

正因为如此。  
For that very reason,  
农业税就该减。

政策上。  
Policy, in turn,  
也要向农业倾斜。  
should tilt in favor of farming.  

他主张按地征税。  
He advocated taxing land according to its condition.  
根据土质的好坏。

来决定税轻税重。

具体做法是。  
The concrete method was this.  
两年征一次税。

收成好的年景。  
In years of good harvest,  
按十取三。  
the rate would be three in ten.  
收成一般。  
In middling years,  
按十取二。  
it would be two in ten.  
收成差。  
In poor years,  
按十取一。  
it would be one in ten.  
若遇灾年。  
And in years of famine or disaster,  
则完全不征。  
no tax would be taken at all.  
这样的税率。  
Rates like these  
已经很低了。

而且他还解释。  
He even explained  
为什么要两年一征。

因为如果某一年。  
If, in a bad year,  
年景不好。  
tax is still demanded,  
还逼着农民交税。  
farmers who cannot pay  
他们交不起。  
will be driven  
就只能去借高利贷。

可如果拖到两年一交。  
But if payment is deferred to every other year,  
他们就可能。  
they may be able  
拿上一年的余粮。  
to use surplus grain from the previous year  
来应付税赋。  
to meet their taxes.  
这样就不必。  
That way they do not need  
被高利贷逼死。

这样的总理。  
A prime minister like that,  
确实是处处。  
truly was thinking  
都在替百姓想。  
at every turn for the people.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`原因状语从句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If, in a bad year, tax is still demanded, farmers who cannot pay will be driven to borrow at usurious interest`。<br>
难点拆解：前半句是条件 `If...tax is still demanded`，说明“年景差还硬征税”的情况；后半句是结果 `farmers ... will be driven...`。其中 `who cannot pay` 是定语成分，补充说明是哪一类农民。<br>
常用短语：
- `tilt in favor of`：向……倾斜
- `meet their taxes`：缴纳税赋

### 第四，包产到户

很多人印象里。

包产到户。

是近现代的发明。  
is a modern invention.  
作者却说。  
The author says no.  
这东西最早。  
Its earliest form  
是管仲提出来的。  
was already there in Guan Zhong.  
道理并不复杂。  
The logic is not complicated.  
如果土地。  
If land and labor  
分到各家各户。  
are assigned down to each household,  
百姓自然就会。  
people naturally  
更紧地盯住农时。  
watch the farming season more closely,  
知道时间紧。  
know how urgent time is,  
也知道饥寒。  
and understand how near hunger and cold can be.  
于是就会。  
Then they rise early,  
早起晚睡。  
sleep late,  
全家一起。  
and work together as a family  
拼命干活。  
with full effort.  
反过来。  
By contrast,  
若不包产到户。  
if land is not assigned in this way,  
地利就用不尽。  
the potential of the land is not fully used,  
人力也发挥不尽。  
and the power of labor is not fully brought out.  
大家容易。  
People easily  
得过且过。  
drift along,  
磨磨蹭蹭。  
dragging their feet.  
可一旦分清了。  
Once shares are clearly divided,  
税率也明白了。  
and tax rates openly fixed,  
多劳多得。  
the more one works, the more one gains.  
百姓自然会。  
The people will naturally  
拼命去种。  
put all their strength into cultivation.  
父子兄弟之间。  
Fathers, sons, and brothers  
也会相互督促。  
will push each other on.  
这套逻辑。  
That logic  
听到今天。  
still sounds

like hard common sense.  

### 第五，兴修水利

管仲说。

善于治国的人。

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`转折并列句`、`原因状语从句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `Qi's treasury had once been fed mainly by Qi's own people. Now it was being fed, in part, by foreigners`。<br>
难点拆解：这里前后是强烈对比，前一句讲过去财政来源，后一句讲改革后的变化；中间隐含的原因是“盐铁专营把成本转嫁给了外部购买者”。这种句子读时要抓住“以前/现在”的对照。<br>
常用短语：
- `under state ownership`：收归国有
- `have no choice but to`：别无选择，只能……

打听之下。  
They learned  
丁家有足够的粮。  
that a certain Ding household had enough grain  
能让大军。  
to feed the army  
吃上五个月。  
for five months.  
于是管仲就去。  
So Guan Zhong went to them  
把那件。  
and offered that  
“无价之宝”。  
"priceless treasure"  
拿去抵押。  
as collateral（抵押品）,  
换他们的粮。  
in exchange for their grain.  
丁家受宠若惊。  
The Ding family was overwhelmed with honor.  
他们把那只龟。  
They received the turtle,  
高高供起来。  
set it up in reverence,  
而齐军出征的粮草。  
while the food problem  
就这样解决了。  
for Qi's campaign was solved just like that.  
这就叫。  
That is what is called  
御神用宝。  
"using a treasure by invoking the gods."  
说白了。  
Put bluntly,  
就是借国家威信。  
it means using the prestige（威信） of the state  
去忽悠老百姓。  
to persuade, or fool, ordinary people.  
作者最后。  
The author ends by joking  
还不忘吐槽一句。  
one last time.  
股市里炒权证。  
Speculation in warrants,  
炒期指。  
speculation in futures,  
很多时候。  
often looks  
不也像是。  
like a variation  
这种玩法的变种吗？  
of the same game.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`宾语从句`、`分词结构`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `They received the turtle, set it up in reverence, while the food problem for Qi's campaign was solved just like that`。<br>
难点拆解：这类叙事句常把多个动作并列推进：`received`、`set up`、`was solved`；其中后半句用了 `while` 带出结果并形成对照，既写丁家受宠若惊，也写齐军粮草问题顺手被解决。<br>
常用短语：
- `leave someone wide-eyed`：让某人听得目瞪口呆
- `just like that`：就这样、一下子
